              ELSE ROUND(COALESCE(p.start_s, 0) / 60.0, 1) END             AS start_gap_minutes,
         CASE WHEN p.na_reason IN ('no_warehouse_events', 'no_query_history') THEN NULL
              ELSE ROUND(COALESCE(p.between_s, 0) / 60.0, 1) END           AS between_queries_minutes,
         -- with auto-stop off nothing waits to stop it: the stretch after the last query is running
         -- with no query, not an auto-stop wait
         CASE WHEN p.na_reason IN ('no_warehouse_events', 'no_query_history') THEN NULL
              WHEN p.auto_stop_minutes = 0 THEN 0
              ELSE ROUND(COALESCE(p.tail_s, 0) / 60.0, 1) END              AS stop_tail_minutes,
         CASE WHEN p.na_reason IN ('no_warehouse_events', 'no_query_history') THEN NULL
              ELSE ROUND((COALESCE(p.no_query_s, 0)
                          + CASE WHEN p.auto_stop_minutes = 0 THEN COALESCE(p.tail_s, 0) ELSE 0 END) / 60.0, 1)
         END                                                               AS no_query_minutes,
         CASE WHEN p.na_reason IS NULL
              THEN ROUND(COALESCE(p.counted_s, 0) * 100.0 / p.running_s, 1) END AS idle_share_pct,
         ROUND(p.up_cluster_s / 60.0, 1)                                   AS up_cluster_minutes,
         CASE WHEN p.na_reason IS NULL
              THEN ROUND(COALESCE(p.idle_cluster_s, 0) / 60.0, 1) END      AS idle_cluster_minutes,
         p.max_clusters_seen,
         ROUND(p.net_dbus, 2)                                              AS net_dbus,
         CASE WHEN p.price_basis IN ('priced', 'free') THEN ROUND(p.usd_all, 2) END AS est_usd_list,
         ROUND(p.wasted_raw, 2)                                            AS est_wasted_usd_list,
         p.price_basis,
         60                                                 AS idle_gap_seconds,
         -- no statement history: no gaps to replay; one busy stretch: no gaps, so 0
         CASE WHEN p.qh_rows > 0 THEN ROUND(COALESCE(p.idle_1_s, 0) / 60.0, 1) END  AS autostop_1_idle_minutes,
         CASE WHEN p.qh_rows > 0 THEN COALESCE(p.starts_1, 0) END                   AS autostop_1_cold_starts,
         CASE WHEN p.qh_rows > 0 THEN ROUND(COALESCE(p.idle_2_s, 0) / 60.0, 1) END  AS autostop_2_idle_minutes,
         CASE WHEN p.qh_rows > 0 THEN COALESCE(p.starts_2, 0) END                   AS autostop_2_cold_starts,
         CASE WHEN p.qh_rows > 0 THEN ROUND(COALESCE(p.idle_5_s, 0) / 60.0, 1) END  AS autostop_5_idle_minutes,
         CASE WHEN p.qh_rows > 0 THEN COALESCE(p.starts_5, 0) END                   AS autostop_5_cold_starts,
         CASE WHEN p.qh_rows > 0 THEN ROUND(COALESCE(p.idle_10_s, 0) / 60.0, 1) END AS autostop_10_idle_minutes,
         CASE WHEN p.qh_rows > 0 THEN COALESCE(p.starts_10, 0) END                  AS autostop_10_cold_starts,
         CASE WHEN p.qh_rows > 0 THEN ROUND(COALESCE(p.idle_30_s, 0) / 60.0, 1) END AS autostop_30_idle_minutes,
         CASE WHEN p.qh_rows > 0 THEN COALESCE(p.starts_30, 0) END                  AS autostop_30_cold_starts,
         CASE WHEN p.qh_rows > 0 AND p.auto_stop_minutes IS NOT NULL
              THEN ROUND(COALESCE(p.idle_now_s, 0) / 60.0, 1) END                    AS autostop_now_idle_minutes,
         CASE WHEN p.qh_rows > 0 AND p.auto_stop_minutes IS NOT NULL
              THEN COALESCE(p.starts_now, 0) END                                     AS autostop_now_cold_starts,
         ROUND(p.usd_per_cluster_minute, 4)                                        AS usd_per_cluster_minute,
         CASE
           WHEN p.na_reason = 'no_warehouse_events' THEN
             'billed in the window but no start or stop events were recorded for it, so its running time cannot be measured; if auto-stop is off it may have run the whole window'
           WHEN p.na_reason = 'no_query_history' THEN
             'no SQL statement history was exported for the window, so time running a query cannot be told apart from idle time'
           WHEN p.na_reason = 'no_running_time_measured' THEN
             'billed in the window, but its events show no running time that can be measured (it was already running when its first recorded event in the window arrived)'
           ELSE
             CONCAT_WS('; ',
               CONCAT('idle ', CAST(ROUND(COALESCE(p.counted_s, 0) / 60.0, 0) AS BIGINT), ' of ',
                      CAST(ROUND(p.running_s / 60.0, 0) AS BIGINT), ' running min (',
                      CAST(ROUND(COALESCE(p.counted_s, 0) * 100.0 / p.running_s, 0) AS BIGINT), '%) in ',
                      COALESCE(p.counted_gaps, 0),
                      CASE WHEN COALESCE(p.counted_gaps, 0) = 1 THEN ' gap' ELSE ' gaps' END,
                      ' longer than ', 60, ' s'),
               CASE WHEN COALESCE(p.counted_gaps, 0) > 0
                    THEN CONCAT('longest ', CAST(ROUND(COALESCE(p.longest_s, 0) / 60.0, 0) AS BIGINT), ' min') END,
               CASE WHEN COALESCE(p.tail_s, 0) > 0
                    THEN CONCAT(CAST(ROUND(COALESCE(p.tail_s, 0) / 60.0, 0) AS BIGINT),
                                CASE WHEN p.auto_stop_minutes = 0 THEN ' min after the last query, auto-stop off'
                                     ELSE ' min after the last query, waiting for auto-stop' END) END,
               CASE WHEN COALESCE(p.no_query_s, 0) > 0
                    THEN CONCAT(CAST(ROUND(COALESCE(p.no_query_s, 0) / 60.0, 0) AS BIGINT), ' min in ',
                                COALESCE(p.no_query_periods, 0),
                                CASE WHEN COALESCE(p.no_query_periods, 0) = 1 THEN ' running period' ELSE ' running periods' END,
                                ' with no query at all') END,
               CASE WHEN p.wasted_raw IS NULL
                    THEN CONCAT('no dollar figure: ',
                                CASE WHEN p.price_basis = 'unpriced' THEN 'its usage has no list price'
                                     ELSE 'no billing rows for it in the window' END) END)
         END                                                                AS waste_reason,
         p.na_reason                                                       AS not_assessed_reason,
         CASE
           WHEN p.na_reason IS NOT NULL THEN 'NOT_ASSESSED'
           -- a NULL (unpriced) wasted_raw never meets a dollar band
           WHEN (COALESCE(p.counted_s, 0) >= 30 * 60
                 AND COALESCE(p.counted_s, 0) * 100.0 / p.running_s >= 60)
                OR p.wasted_raw >= 200 THEN 'CRITICAL'
           WHEN (COALESCE(p.counted_s, 0) >= 30 * 60
                 AND COALESCE(p.counted_s, 0) * 100.0 / p.running_s >= 30)
                OR p.wasted_raw >= 20 THEN 'WARN'
           ELSE 'OK'
         END                                                                AS status
  FROM priced p
  ORDER BY CASE status WHEN 'CRITICAL' THEN 0 WHEN 'WARN' THEN 1 WHEN 'NOT_ASSESSED' THEN 2 ELSE 3 END,
           est_wasted_usd_list DESC NULLS LAST, counted_idle_minutes DESC NULLS LAST,
           workspace_id, warehouse_id
  ) q
),
-- Workspace id to name.
ws AS (
  SELECT CAST(workspace_id AS STRING) AS workspace_id, workspace_name
  FROM system.access.workspaces_latest
),
-- Warehouse id to name: the latest row per warehouse.
wh AS (
  SELECT CAST(warehouse_id AS STRING) AS warehouse_id, warehouse_name FROM system.compute.warehouses
  QUALIFY ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY warehouse_id ORDER BY change_time DESC) = 1
),
-- Warehouses that ran in the window, with workspace and warehouse names.
w AS (
  SELECT i.*, COALESCE(ws.workspace_name, CAST(i.workspace_id AS STRING), 'Account-level') AS workspace,
         concat(COALESCE(wh.warehouse_name, CAST(i.warehouse_id AS STRING)), ' · ', COALESCE(ws.workspace_name, CAST(i.workspace_id AS STRING), 'Account-level')) AS warehouse
  FROM idle i
  LEFT JOIN wh ON wh.warehouse_id = CAST(i.warehouse_id AS STRING)
  LEFT JOIN ws ON ws.workspace_id = CAST(i.workspace_id AS STRING)
  WHERE i.running_minutes > 0
),
-- One row per warehouse and shorter setting it could take: serverless from 1 min, pro and classic from 10.
replay AS (
  SELECT w.*, o.minutes AS setting,
         CASE o.minutes WHEN 1 THEN autostop_1_idle_minutes WHEN 2 THEN autostop_2_idle_minutes
                        WHEN 5 THEN autostop_5_idle_minutes ELSE autostop_10_idle_minutes END AS at_idle_minutes,
         CASE o.minutes WHEN 1 THEN autostop_1_cold_starts WHEN 2 THEN autostop_2_cold_starts
                        WHEN 5 THEN autostop_5_cold_starts ELSE autostop_10_cold_starts END AS at_cold_starts
  FROM w CROSS JOIN (VALUES (1), (2), (5), (10)) AS o(minutes)
  WHERE w.not_assessed_reason IS NULL AND w.autostop_now_idle_minutes IS NOT NULL
    AND w.usd_per_cluster_minute IS NOT NULL AND w.auto_stop_minutes IS NOT NULL
    AND (w.warehouse_kind = 'serverless' OR o.minutes >= 10)
    AND (w.auto_stop_minutes = 0 OR o.minutes < w.auto_stop_minutes)
),
-- Per row: $ saved, idle hours saved, extra cold starts, and the warehouse's lowest setting.
whatif AS (
  SELECT *,
         GREATEST(autostop_now_idle_minutes - at_idle_minutes, 0) * usd_per_cluster_minute AS saving_usd,
         GREATEST(autostop_now_idle_minutes - at_idle_minutes, 0) / 60.0 AS saved_hours,
         GREATEST(COALESCE(at_cold_starts, 0) - COALESCE(autostop_now_cold_starts, 0), 0) AS extra_starts,
         MIN(setting) OVER (PARTITION BY workspace_id, warehouse_id) AS lowest_setting
  FROM replay
  WHERE at_idle_minutes IS NOT NULL
),
-- Each setting's extra saving over the next longer one (now, 10, 5, 2, 1 min): the steps add up.
steps AS (
  -- each setting's extra saving over the next longer one, so a warehouse's steps add up to its best saving
  SELECT *, saving_usd - COALESCE(LAG(saving_usd) OVER (PARTITION BY workspace_id, warehouse_id ORDER BY setting DESC), 0)
              AS step_saving_usd
  FROM whatif
),
-- Warehouses ranked by their best saving, for the top 10.
ranked AS (
  SELECT *, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY best_saving DESC, workspace_id, warehouse_id) AS rk
  FROM (SELECT *, MAX(saving_usd) OVER (PARTITION BY workspace_id, warehouse_id) AS best_saving FROM steps)
)
-- Final SELECT: the rows the widgets read.
SELECT warehouse, workspace, warehouse_kind AS kind, setting,
       concat('Down to ', lpad(CAST(setting AS STRING), 2, '0'), ' min') AS step,
       ROUND(step_saving_usd, 2) AS step_saving,
       lpad(CAST(setting AS STRING), 2, '0') || ' min' AS setting_label,
       ROUND(saving_usd, 2) AS saving, ROUND(saved_hours, 1) AS hours_saved, extra_starts,
       CASE WHEN rk <= 10 THEN warehouse ELSE 'All others' END AS top_warehouse,
       CASE WHEN warehouse_kind = 'serverless' AND setting = 1 THEN saving_usd END AS saving_serverless_1,
       CASE WHEN warehouse_kind = 'serverless' AND setting = 2 THEN saving_usd END AS saving_serverless_2,
       CASE WHEN warehouse_kind = 'serverless' AND setting = 5 THEN saving_usd END AS saving_serverless_5,
       CASE WHEN warehouse_kind <> 'serverless' AND setting = 10 THEN saving_usd END AS saving_classic_10,
       CASE WHEN warehouse_kind = 'serverless' AND setting = 1 THEN extra_starts END AS starts_serverless_1,
       CASE WHEN warehouse_kind = 'serverless' AND setting = 2 THEN extra_starts END AS starts_serverless_2,
       CASE WHEN warehouse_kind = 'serverless' AND setting = 5 THEN extra_starts END AS starts_serverless_5,
       CASE WHEN warehouse_kind <> 'serverless' AND setting = 10 THEN extra_starts END AS starts_classic_10
FROM ranked
ORDER BY saving_usd DESC

Clusters: idle nodes and auto-termination, one row per cluster

Shows: Per all-purpose (and other non-job) cluster: its auto-termination setting and band, whether it ran, its idle-node ratio, average CPU, idle $ and total $. Used by: The Clusters & pools page, except the pool tile and table.

Why it is built this way: An all-purpose cluster with machines at near 0% CPU is paying for nothing; per-minute node use (node_timeline) shows how much, and the auto-termination setting shows why it stayed up.

How its tables join

Grey: system tables. Blue border: a shared check (its steps are listed under Steps). White: steps of the query. Green: the rows the dashboard shows. ◆ marks the columns the joins use.

  1. classic_clusters_config_current → c: the check's rows
  2. compute_idle_node_ratio → j: the check's rows
  3. ws → named: LEFT JOIN ON workspace_id: names

1. System tables it reads

  • system.access.workspaces_latest
  • system.billing.list_prices
  • system.billing.usage
  • system.compute.clusters
  • system.compute.node_timeline

2. Steps (CTEs), in order

  1. nodes: The shared check compute_idle_node_ratio. Idle cluster nodes: per cluster, how much node time had almost no CPU in use, and that time's $.
    1. price: List price of every SKU over time, with the date each price really ended.
    2. cost_rollup: Per cluster: DBUs and $ billed in the window.
    3. finding: Per cluster, from per-minute node data: node-minutes, and those with CPU under 5% (workers, driver).
    4. judged: Idle is judged on the workers; a single-node cluster is judged on its driver.
    Its SQL (136 lines)
    -- Clusters: idle nodes and auto-termination, one row per cluster. List price; dates from the Time Window.
-- The shared check compute_idle_node_ratio. Idle cluster nodes: per cluster, how much node time had almost no CPU in use, and that time's $.
nodes AS (
  SELECT datediff(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), :time_range.min) AS window_days, q.*
  FROM (
  -- List price of every SKU over time, with the date each price really ended.
  WITH price AS (
      SELECT sku_name, cloud, usage_unit, price_start_time, price_end_time,
             CAST(pricing.effective_list.default AS DOUBLE) AS list_rate
      FROM 
  (
      SELECT account_id, sku_name, cloud, currency_code, usage_unit, pricing,
             price_start_time, effective_end AS price_end_time
      FROM (
          SELECT account_id, sku_name, cloud, currency_code, usage_unit, pricing,
                 price_start_time, price_end_time, next_start_time,
                 -- CASE, not LEAST, so a NULL end/next behaves the same on DuckDB and Databricks.
                 CASE
                     WHEN price_end_time IS NULL THEN next_start_time
                     WHEN next_start_time IS NULL THEN price_end_time
                     WHEN price_end_time <= next_start_time THEN price_end_time
                     ELSE next_start_time
                 END AS effective_end
          FROM (
              SELECT account_id, sku_name, cloud, currency_code, usage_unit, pricing,
                     price_start_time, price_end_time,
                     LEAD(price_start_time) OVER (
                         PARTITION BY sku_name, cloud, usage_unit
                         ORDER BY price_start_time, price_end_time NULLS LAST
                     ) AS next_start_time
              FROM `system`.`billing`.`list_prices`
              WHERE currency_code = 'USD'
          ) ranked
      ) capped
      WHERE effective_end IS NULL OR effective_end > price_start_time
  )
   list_prices
  ),
  -- Per cluster: DBUs and $ billed in the window.
  cost_rollup AS (
      -- Pre-aggregated per cluster_id (globally-unique GUID -> workspace_id not needed for the join).
      SELECT u.usage_metadata.cluster_id                         AS cluster_id,
             SUM(u.usage_quantity)                               AS net_dbus,
             SUM(u.usage_quantity * COALESCE(p.list_rate, 0))    AS est_usd_list,
             -- price_basis: a real $0 (free-usage SKU) vs a pricing-coverage gap (understates cost).
             CASE
               WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN p.list_rate IS NULL AND upper(u.sku_name) NOT LIKE '%FREE_USAGE%'
                             THEN u.usage_quantity ELSE 0 END) > 0 THEN 'unpriced'
               WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN p.list_rate IS NOT NULL THEN u.usage_quantity ELSE 0 END) = 0 THEN 'free'
               ELSE 'priced'
             END                                                  AS price_basis
      FROM `system`.`billing`.`usage` u
      LEFT JOIN price p
        ON u.sku_name = p.sku_name AND u.cloud = p.cloud AND u.usage_unit = p.usage_unit
       AND u.usage_end_time >= p.price_start_time
       AND (p.price_end_time IS NULL OR u.usage_end_time < p.price_end_time)
      WHERE upper(u.usage_unit) = 'DBU'
        AND u.usage_metadata.cluster_id IS NOT NULL
        AND u.usage_date >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE)
        AND u.usage_date <  date_add(:time_range.max, 1)
      GROUP BY u.usage_metadata.cluster_id
  ),
  -- Per cluster, from per-minute node data: node-minutes, and those with CPU under 5% (workers, driver).
  finding AS (
      SELECT
          cluster_id,
          -- lexicographically-largest node_type label (representative only, not load-weighted)
          MAX(node_type)                                                       AS node_type,
          COUNT(*)                                                             AS total_slices,
          SUM(CASE WHEN NOT driver THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)                          AS worker_slices,
          -- idle = CPU busy (user+system) below 5 on that minute-slice, worker-only.
          SUM(CASE WHEN NOT driver AND (cpu_user_percent + cpu_system_percent) < 5
                   THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)                                          AS worker_idle_slices,
          SUM(CASE WHEN driver THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)                              AS driver_slices,
          -- driver fallback for a genuine single-node cluster (no worker slices at all)
          SUM(CASE WHEN driver AND (cpu_user_percent + cpu_system_percent) < 5
                   THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)                                          AS driver_idle_slices,
          COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN driver THEN NULL ELSE node_type END)        AS worker_node_type_variants,
          -- utilization averaged over ALL slices (NOT only idle rows) so the mean is honest.
          AVG(cpu_user_percent + cpu_system_percent)                          AS avg_cpu_pct_all,
          MAX(cpu_user_percent + cpu_system_percent)                          AS peak_cpu_pct_all,
          AVG(mem_used_percent)                                               AS avg_mem_pct_all,
          MAX(mem_used_percent)                                               AS peak_mem_pct_all,
          MIN(start_time)                                                     AS first_slice,
          MAX(end_time)                                                       AS last_slice
      FROM `system`.`compute`.`node_timeline`
      WHERE start_time >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE)
        AND start_time < date_add(:time_range.max, 1)   -- billing stops before today too
      GROUP BY cluster_id
  ),
  -- Idle is judged on the workers; a single-node cluster is judged on its driver.
  judged AS (
      -- worker slices judge idle when there are any; a single-node cluster (no workers at all)
      -- falls back to its driver instead of being silently dropped.
      SELECT f.*,
             CASE WHEN f.worker_slices > 0 THEN f.worker_slices ELSE f.driver_slices END AS judged_slices,
             CASE WHEN f.worker_slices > 0 THEN f.worker_idle_slices ELSE f.driver_idle_slices END AS judged_idle_slices
      FROM finding f
  )
  SELECT
      f.cluster_id,
      f.node_type,
      f.total_slices,
      f.judged_idle_slices                                                  AS idle_slices,
      -- idle_ratio = idle minute-slices / judged minute-slices (worker-only, or driver on a
      -- single-node cluster) - a fraction 0-1, never "hours".
      f.judged_idle_slices / NULLIF(f.judged_slices, 0)                     AS idle_ratio,
      f.worker_node_type_variants,
      f.avg_cpu_pct_all,
      f.peak_cpu_pct_all,
      f.avg_mem_pct_all,
      f.peak_mem_pct_all,
      f.first_slice,
      f.last_slice,
      COALESCE(cr.net_dbus, 0)     AS net_dbus,
      COALESCE(cr.est_usd_list, 0) AS est_usd_list,
      COALESCE(cr.price_basis, 'priced') AS price_basis,
      -- directional wasted-$ overlay: idle worker minutes over ALL node minutes, so the driver's
      -- share of the bill is never counted as idle worker waste.
      COALESCE(cr.est_usd_list, 0) * (f.judged_idle_slices / NULLIF(
        CASE WHEN f.worker_slices > 0 THEN f.worker_slices + f.driver_slices ELSE f.driver_slices END, 0)) AS est_wasted_usd_list,
      -- status: worst-first band on idle_ratio (field heuristic). Too few judged slices -> NOT_ASSESSED.
      CASE
        WHEN f.judged_slices < 60                                                THEN 'NOT_ASSESSED'
        WHEN f.judged_idle_slices / NULLIF(f.judged_slices, 0) >= 0.8       THEN 'CRITICAL'
        WHEN f.judged_idle_slices / NULLIF(f.judged_slices, 0) >= 0.5       THEN 'WARN'
        ELSE 'OK'
      END AS status
  FROM judged f
  LEFT JOIN cost_rollup cr
    ON f.cluster_id = cr.cluster_id
  ORDER BY CASE status WHEN 'CRITICAL' THEN 0 WHEN 'WARN' THEN 1 WHEN 'OK' THEN 2 ELSE 3 END,
           est_wasted_usd_list DESC
  LIMIT 100000
  ) q
)
  2. cfg: The shared check classic_clusters_config_current. Cluster settings: each classic cluster's current configuration, incl. auto-termination.
    Its SQL (20 lines)
    -- The shared check classic_clusters_config_current. Cluster settings: each classic cluster's current configuration, incl. auto-termination.
cfg AS (
  SELECT 0 AS window_days, q.*
  FROM (
  SELECT cluster_id,
         cluster_name AS cluster_name,
         owned_by AS owned_by,
         driver_node_type, worker_node_type, worker_count,
         min_autoscale_workers, max_autoscale_workers, auto_termination_minutes, enable_elastic_disk,
         cluster_source, dbr_version, data_security_mode, policy_id, driver_instance_pool_id,
         worker_instance_pool_id, tags, init_scripts, aws_attributes, azure_attributes, gcp_attributes,
         create_time, delete_time, change_time, workspace_id, account_id
  FROM (
    SELECT *, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY cluster_id ORDER BY change_time DESC) AS rn
    FROM `system`.`compute`.`clusters`
  )
  WHERE rn = 1 AND delete_time IS NULL
  ORDER BY cluster_id
  ) q
)
  3. ws: Workspace id to name.
    Its SQL (5 lines)
    -- Workspace id to name.
ws AS (
  SELECT CAST(workspace_id AS STRING) AS workspace_id, workspace_name
  FROM system.access.workspaces_latest
)
  4. c: Each cluster's current settings, deleted ones left out.
    Its SQL (5 lines)
    -- Each cluster's current settings, deleted ones left out.
c AS (
  SELECT * FROM cfg WHERE delete_time IS NULL
  QUALIFY ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY cluster_id ORDER BY change_time DESC) = 1
)
  5. j: Settings joined to the idle-node check; FULL OUTER keeps clusters that didn't run and ones with no settings row.
    Its SQL (7 lines)
    -- Settings joined to the idle-node check; FULL OUTER keeps clusters that didn't run and ones with no settings row.
j AS (
  SELECT COALESCE(CAST(c.cluster_id AS STRING), CAST(n.cluster_id AS STRING)) AS cluster_id,
         c.cluster_name, CAST(c.workspace_id AS STRING) AS workspace_id, c.cluster_source, c.auto_termination_minutes,
         n.total_slices, n.idle_ratio, n.avg_cpu_pct_all, n.est_usd_list, n.est_wasted_usd_list, n.status
  FROM c FULL OUTER JOIN nodes n ON CAST(n.cluster_id AS STRING) = CAST(c.cluster_id AS STRING)
)
  6. named: Workspace name, kind (all-purpose, job, ...) and whether it ran.
    Its SQL (8 lines)
    -- Workspace name, kind (all-purpose, job, ...) and whether it ran.
named AS (
  SELECT j.*, COALESCE(ws.workspace_name, CAST(j.workspace_id AS STRING), 'Account-level') AS workspace,
         CASE WHEN cluster_source IN ('UI', 'API') THEN 'All-purpose'
              WHEN cluster_source = 'JOB' THEN 'Job' ELSE COALESCE(initcap(cluster_source), 'Unknown') END AS cluster_kind,
         COALESCE(total_slices, 0) > 0 AS has_run
  FROM j LEFT JOIN ws ON ws.workspace_id = j.workspace_id
)
  7. ranked: Job clusters left out; the rest ranked by idle ratio for the top 10.
    Its SQL (6 lines)
    -- Job clusters left out; the rest ranked by idle ratio for the top 10.
ranked AS (
  SELECT *, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY CASE WHEN has_run AND total_slices >= 60 THEN idle_ratio END DESC NULLS LAST,
                                        cluster_id) AS rk
  FROM named WHERE cluster_kind <> 'Job'
)

3. Final query

One row per cluster; top_cluster names the 10 with the highest idle ratio (with enough data to judge).

4. Columns it returns

ColumnMeaning
clusterThe cluster's name and id.
workspaceThe workspace's name (its id when the name is unknown).
kindAll-purpose, or another source (job clusters are left out).
statusCRITICAL, WARN, OK or NOT_ASSESSED (not enough data to judge), from the rule in Rules.
clustersAlways 1: charts add it up to count clusters.
ranRan, or Did not run in the window.
auto_terminationIts auto-termination in minutes; off when it never stops.
autoterm_bandThe setting grouped for the charts.
no_autoterm1 for an all-purpose cluster with auto-termination off.
idle_pctShare of its node time with CPU near 0%.
avg_cpu_pctAverage CPU over its nodes.
idle_usdThe idle node time's $ at list price.
flagged_idle_usdidle_usd when the cluster is Critical or Warn.
total_usdAll its $ at list price.
top_clusterThe name for the 10 with the highest idle share and enough data.
SQL with comments (195 lines)
-- Clusters: idle nodes and auto-termination, one row per cluster. List price; dates from the Time Window.
-- The shared check compute_idle_node_ratio. Idle cluster nodes: per cluster, how much node time had almost no CPU in use, and that time's $.
WITH nodes AS (
  SELECT datediff(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), :time_range.min) AS window_days, q.*
  FROM (
  -- List price of every SKU over time, with the date each price really ended.
  WITH price AS (
      SELECT sku_name, cloud, usage_unit, price_start_time, price_end_time,
             CAST(pricing.effective_list.default AS DOUBLE) AS list_rate
      FROM 
  (
      SELECT account_id, sku_name, cloud, currency_code, usage_unit, pricing,
             price_start_time, effective_end AS price_end_time
      FROM (
          SELECT account_id, sku_name, cloud, currency_code, usage_unit, pricing,
                 price_start_time, price_end_time, next_start_time,
                 -- CASE, not LEAST, so a NULL end/next behaves the same on DuckDB and Databricks.
                 CASE
                     WHEN price_end_time IS NULL THEN next_start_time
                     WHEN next_start_time IS NULL THEN price_end_time
                     WHEN price_end_time <= next_start_time THEN price_end_time
                     ELSE next_start_time
                 END AS effective_end
          FROM (
              SELECT account_id, sku_name, cloud, currency_code, usage_unit, pricing,
                     price_start_time, price_end_time,
                     LEAD(price_start_time) OVER (
                         PARTITION BY sku_name, cloud, usage_unit
                         ORDER BY price_start_time, price_end_time NULLS LAST
                     ) AS next_start_time
              FROM `system`.`billing`.`list_prices`
              WHERE currency_code = 'USD'
          ) ranked
      ) capped
      WHERE effective_end IS NULL OR effective_end > price_start_time
  )
   list_prices
  ),
  -- Per cluster: DBUs and $ billed in the window.
  cost_rollup AS (
      -- Pre-aggregated per cluster_id (globally-unique GUID -> workspace_id not needed for the join).
      SELECT u.usage_metadata.cluster_id                         AS cluster_id,
             SUM(u.usage_quantity)                               AS net_dbus,
             SUM(u.usage_quantity * COALESCE(p.list_rate, 0))    AS est_usd_list,
             -- price_basis: a real $0 (free-usage SKU) vs a pricing-coverage gap (understates cost).
             CASE
               WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN p.list_rate IS NULL AND upper(u.sku_name) NOT LIKE '%FREE_USAGE%'
                             THEN u.usage_quantity ELSE 0 END) > 0 THEN 'unpriced'
               WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN p.list_rate IS NOT NULL THEN u.usage_quantity ELSE 0 END) = 0 THEN 'free'
               ELSE 'priced'
             END                                                  AS price_basis
      FROM `system`.`billing`.`usage` u
      LEFT JOIN price p
        ON u.sku_name = p.sku_name AND u.cloud = p.cloud AND u.usage_unit = p.usage_unit
       AND u.usage_end_time >= p.price_start_time
       AND (p.price_end_time IS NULL OR u.usage_end_time < p.price_end_time)
      WHERE upper(u.usage_unit) = 'DBU'
        AND u.usage_metadata.cluster_id IS NOT NULL
        AND u.usage_date >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE)
        AND u.usage_date <  date_add(:time_range.max, 1)
      GROUP BY u.usage_metadata.cluster_id
  ),
  -- Per cluster, from per-minute node data: node-minutes, and those with CPU under 5% (workers, driver).
  finding AS (
      SELECT
          cluster_id,
          -- lexicographically-largest node_type label (representative only, not load-weighted)
          MAX(node_type)                                                       AS node_type,
          COUNT(*)                                                             AS total_slices,
          SUM(CASE WHEN NOT driver THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)                          AS worker_slices,
          -- idle = CPU busy (user+system) below 5 on that minute-slice, worker-only.
          SUM(CASE WHEN NOT driver AND (cpu_user_percent + cpu_system_percent) < 5
                   THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)                                          AS worker_idle_slices,
          SUM(CASE WHEN driver THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)                              AS driver_slices,
          -- driver fallback for a genuine single-node cluster (no worker slices at all)
          SUM(CASE WHEN driver AND (cpu_user_percent + cpu_system_percent) < 5
                   THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)                                          AS driver_idle_slices,
          COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN driver THEN NULL ELSE node_type END)        AS worker_node_type_variants,
          -- utilization averaged over ALL slices (NOT only idle rows) so the mean is honest.
          AVG(cpu_user_percent + cpu_system_percent)                          AS avg_cpu_pct_all,
          MAX(cpu_user_percent + cpu_system_percent)                          AS peak_cpu_pct_all,
          AVG(mem_used_percent)                                               AS avg_mem_pct_all,
          MAX(mem_used_percent)                                               AS peak_mem_pct_all,
          MIN(start_time)                                                     AS first_slice,
          MAX(end_time)                                                       AS last_slice
      FROM `system`.`compute`.`node_timeline`
      WHERE start_time >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE)
        AND start_time < date_add(:time_range.max, 1)   -- billing stops before today too
      GROUP BY cluster_id
  ),
  -- Idle is judged on the workers; a single-node cluster is judged on its driver.
  judged AS (
      -- worker slices judge idle when there are any; a single-node cluster (no workers at all)
      -- falls back to its driver instead of being silently dropped.
      SELECT f.*,
             CASE WHEN f.worker_slices > 0 THEN f.worker_slices ELSE f.driver_slices END AS judged_slices,
             CASE WHEN f.worker_slices > 0 THEN f.worker_idle_slices ELSE f.driver_idle_slices END AS judged_idle_slices
      FROM finding f
  )
  SELECT
      f.cluster_id,
      f.node_type,
      f.total_slices,
      f.judged_idle_slices                                                  AS idle_slices,
      -- idle_ratio = idle minute-slices / judged minute-slices (worker-only, or driver on a
      -- single-node cluster) - a fraction 0-1, never "hours".
      f.judged_idle_slices / NULLIF(f.judged_slices, 0)                     AS idle_ratio,
      f.worker_node_type_variants,
      f.avg_cpu_pct_all,
      f.peak_cpu_pct_all,
      f.avg_mem_pct_all,
      f.peak_mem_pct_all,
      f.first_slice,
      f.last_slice,
      COALESCE(cr.net_dbus, 0)     AS net_dbus,
      COALESCE(cr.est_usd_list, 0) AS est_usd_list,
      COALESCE(cr.price_basis, 'priced') AS price_basis,
      -- directional wasted-$ overlay: idle worker minutes over ALL node minutes, so the driver's
      -- share of the bill is never counted as idle worker waste.
      COALESCE(cr.est_usd_list, 0) * (f.judged_idle_slices / NULLIF(
        CASE WHEN f.worker_slices > 0 THEN f.worker_slices + f.driver_slices ELSE f.driver_slices END, 0)) AS est_wasted_usd_list,
      -- status: worst-first band on idle_ratio (field heuristic). Too few judged slices -> NOT_ASSESSED.
      CASE
        WHEN f.judged_slices < 60                                                THEN 'NOT_ASSESSED'
        WHEN f.judged_idle_slices / NULLIF(f.judged_slices, 0) >= 0.8       THEN 'CRITICAL'
        WHEN f.judged_idle_slices / NULLIF(f.judged_slices, 0) >= 0.5       THEN 'WARN'
        ELSE 'OK'
      END AS status
  FROM judged f
  LEFT JOIN cost_rollup cr
    ON f.cluster_id = cr.cluster_id
  ORDER BY CASE status WHEN 'CRITICAL' THEN 0 WHEN 'WARN' THEN 1 WHEN 'OK' THEN 2 ELSE 3 END,
           est_wasted_usd_list DESC
  LIMIT 100000
  ) q
),
-- The shared check classic_clusters_config_current. Cluster settings: each classic cluster's current configuration, incl. auto-termination.
cfg AS (
  SELECT 0 AS window_days, q.*
  FROM (
  SELECT cluster_id,
         cluster_name AS cluster_name,
         owned_by AS owned_by,
         driver_node_type, worker_node_type, worker_count,
         min_autoscale_workers, max_autoscale_workers, auto_termination_minutes, enable_elastic_disk,
         cluster_source, dbr_version, data_security_mode, policy_id, driver_instance_pool_id,
         worker_instance_pool_id, tags, init_scripts, aws_attributes, azure_attributes, gcp_attributes,
         create_time, delete_time, change_time, workspace_id, account_id
  FROM (
    SELECT *, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY cluster_id ORDER BY change_time DESC) AS rn
    FROM `system`.`compute`.`clusters`
  )
  WHERE rn = 1 AND delete_time IS NULL
  ORDER BY cluster_id
  ) q
),
-- Workspace id to name.
ws AS (
  SELECT CAST(workspace_id AS STRING) AS workspace_id, workspace_name
  FROM system.access.workspaces_latest
),
-- Each cluster's current settings, deleted ones left out.
c AS (
  SELECT * FROM cfg WHERE delete_time IS NULL
  QUALIFY ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY cluster_id ORDER BY change_time DESC) = 1
),
-- Settings joined to the idle-node check; FULL OUTER keeps clusters that didn't run and ones with no settings row.
j AS (
  SELECT COALESCE(CAST(c.cluster_id AS STRING), CAST(n.cluster_id AS STRING)) AS cluster_id,
         c.cluster_name, CAST(c.workspace_id AS STRING) AS workspace_id, c.cluster_source, c.auto_termination_minutes,
         n.total_slices, n.idle_ratio, n.avg_cpu_pct_all, n.est_usd_list, n.est_wasted_usd_list, n.status
  FROM c FULL OUTER JOIN nodes n ON CAST(n.cluster_id AS STRING) = CAST(c.cluster_id AS STRING)
),
-- Workspace name, kind (all-purpose, job, ...) and whether it ran.
named AS (
  SELECT j.*, COALESCE(ws.workspace_name, CAST(j.workspace_id AS STRING), 'Account-level') AS workspace,
         CASE WHEN cluster_source IN ('UI', 'API') THEN 'All-purpose'
              WHEN cluster_source = 'JOB' THEN 'Job' ELSE COALESCE(initcap(cluster_source), 'Unknown') END AS cluster_kind,
         COALESCE(total_slices, 0) > 0 AS has_run
  FROM j LEFT JOIN ws ON ws.workspace_id = j.workspace_id
),
-- Job clusters left out; the rest ranked by idle ratio for the top 10.
ranked AS (
  SELECT *, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY CASE WHEN has_run AND total_slices >= 60 THEN idle_ratio END DESC NULLS LAST,
                                        cluster_id) AS rk
  FROM named WHERE cluster_kind <> 'Job'
)
-- Final SELECT: the rows the widgets read.
SELECT concat(COALESCE(cluster_name, cluster_id), ' (', cluster_id, ')') AS cluster,
       workspace, cluster_kind AS kind, status, 1 AS clusters,
       CASE WHEN has_run THEN 'Ran' ELSE 'Did not run' END AS ran,
       CASE WHEN auto_termination_minutes IS NULL THEN 'unknown' WHEN auto_termination_minutes = 0 THEN 'off'
            ELSE CAST(auto_termination_minutes AS STRING) END AS auto_termination,
       CASE WHEN auto_termination_minutes IS NULL THEN 'Unknown' WHEN auto_termination_minutes = 0 THEN 'Off'
            WHEN auto_termination_minutes <= 15 THEN '015 min or less' WHEN auto_termination_minutes <= 30 THEN '016-030 min'
            WHEN auto_termination_minutes <= 60 THEN '031-060 min' WHEN auto_termination_minutes <= 120 THEN '061-120 min'
            ELSE '121+ min' END AS autoterm_band,
       CASE WHEN cluster_kind = 'All-purpose' AND auto_termination_minutes = 0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS no_autoterm,
       ROUND(idle_ratio * 100, 1) AS idle_pct,
       ROUND(avg_cpu_pct_all, 1) AS avg_cpu_pct,
       est_wasted_usd_list AS idle_usd,
       CASE WHEN status IN ('CRITICAL', 'WARN') THEN est_wasted_usd_list END AS flagged_idle_usd,
       est_usd_list AS total_usd,
       CASE WHEN rk <= 10 AND has_run AND total_slices >= 60
            THEN concat(COALESCE(cluster_name, cluster_id), ' · ', workspace) END AS top_cluster
FROM ranked
ORDER BY idle_usd DESC NULLS LAST

Instance pools: idle instances kept warm

Shows: Each instance pool's standing idle floor (min idle instances) and its $. Used by: The pool tile and table on Clusters & pools.

Why it is built this way: Min idle instances are machines kept running for faster starts; they cost cloud money all the time.

How its tables join

Grey: system tables. Blue border: a shared check (its steps are listed under Steps). White: steps of the query. Green: the rows the dashboard shows. ◆ marks the columns the joins use.

  1. instance_pools_idle_capacity → p: the check's rows
  2. ws → p: LEFT JOIN ON workspace_id: names

1. System tables it reads

  • system.access.workspaces_latest
  • system.billing.list_prices
  • system.billing.usage
  • system.compute.instance_pools

2. Steps (CTEs), in order

  1. pools: The shared check instance_pools_idle_capacity. Instance pools: per pool, the idle instances it keeps warm and its $.
    1. price: List price of every SKU over time, with the date each price really ended.
    2. cost_rollup: Per pool: DBUs and $ billed in the window.
    Its SQL (88 lines)
    -- Instance pools: idle instances kept warm. List price; dates from the Time Window.
-- The shared check instance_pools_idle_capacity. Instance pools: per pool, the idle instances it keeps warm and its $.
pools AS (
  SELECT datediff(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), :time_range.min) AS window_days, q.*
  FROM (
  -- List price of every SKU over time, with the date each price really ended.
  WITH price AS (
    SELECT sku_name, cloud, usage_unit, price_start_time, price_end_time,
           CAST(pricing.effective_list.default AS DOUBLE) AS list_rate
    FROM 
  (
      SELECT account_id, sku_name, cloud, currency_code, usage_unit, pricing,
             price_start_time, effective_end AS price_end_time
      FROM (
          SELECT account_id, sku_name, cloud, currency_code, usage_unit, pricing,
                 price_start_time, price_end_time, next_start_time,
                 -- CASE, not LEAST, so a NULL end/next behaves the same on DuckDB and Databricks.
                 CASE
                     WHEN price_end_time IS NULL THEN next_start_time
                     WHEN next_start_time IS NULL THEN price_end_time
                     WHEN price_end_time <= next_start_time THEN price_end_time
                     ELSE next_start_time
                 END AS effective_end
          FROM (
              SELECT account_id, sku_name, cloud, currency_code, usage_unit, pricing,
                     price_start_time, price_end_time,
                     LEAD(price_start_time) OVER (
                         PARTITION BY sku_name, cloud, usage_unit
                         ORDER BY price_start_time, price_end_time NULLS LAST
                     ) AS next_start_time
              FROM `system`.`billing`.`list_prices`
              WHERE currency_code = 'USD'
          ) ranked
      ) capped
      WHERE effective_end IS NULL OR effective_end > price_start_time
  )
   list_prices
  ),
  -- Per pool: DBUs and $ billed in the window.
  cost_rollup AS (
    SELECT u.workspace_id,
           u.usage_metadata.instance_pool_id AS instance_pool_id,
           SUM(u.usage_quantity)                            AS net_dbus,
           SUM(u.usage_quantity * COALESCE(p.list_rate, 0)) AS est_usd_list,
           CASE
             WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN p.list_rate IS NULL AND upper(u.sku_name) NOT LIKE '%FREE_USAGE%'
                           THEN u.usage_quantity ELSE 0 END) > 0 THEN 'unpriced'
             WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN p.list_rate IS NOT NULL THEN u.usage_quantity ELSE 0 END) = 0 THEN 'free'
             ELSE 'priced'
           END                                               AS price_basis
    FROM `system`.`billing`.`usage` u
    LEFT JOIN price p
      ON u.sku_name = p.sku_name AND u.cloud = p.cloud AND u.usage_unit = p.usage_unit
     AND u.usage_end_time >= p.price_start_time
     AND (p.price_end_time IS NULL OR u.usage_end_time < p.price_end_time)
    WHERE upper(u.usage_unit) = 'DBU'
      AND u.usage_metadata.instance_pool_id IS NOT NULL
      AND u.usage_date >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE)
      AND u.usage_date <  date_add(:time_range.max, 1)
    GROUP BY u.workspace_id, u.usage_metadata.instance_pool_id
  )
  SELECT p.instance_pool_id,
         p.instance_pool_name AS instance_pool_name,
         p.node_type, p.min_idle_instances, p.max_capacity,
         p.idle_instance_autotermination_minutes, p.enable_elastic_disk, p.preloaded_spark_version,
         p.preloaded_docker_images, p.tags, p.aws_attributes, p.azure_attributes, p.gcp_attributes, p.disk_spec,
         p.create_time, p.delete_time, p.change_time, p.workspace_id, p.account_id,
         COALESCE(cr.net_dbus, 0)     AS net_dbus,
         COALESCE(cr.est_usd_list, 0) AS est_usd_list,
         COALESCE(cr.price_basis, 'priced') AS price_basis,
         -- status: worst-first band on the standing idle-instance floor (field heuristic; 5 / 20).
         CASE
           WHEN p.min_idle_instances IS NULL THEN 'NOT_ASSESSED'
           WHEN p.min_idle_instances >= 20 THEN 'CRITICAL'
           WHEN p.min_idle_instances >= 5 THEN 'WARN'
           ELSE 'OK'
         END AS status
  FROM (
    SELECT *, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY instance_pool_id ORDER BY change_time DESC) AS rn
    FROM `system`.`compute`.`instance_pools`
  ) p
  LEFT JOIN cost_rollup cr
    ON cr.workspace_id = p.workspace_id
   AND cr.instance_pool_id = p.instance_pool_id
  WHERE p.rn = 1 AND p.delete_time IS NULL
  ORDER BY p.min_idle_instances DESC
  ) q
)
  2. ws: Workspace id to name.
    Its SQL (5 lines)
    -- Workspace id to name.
ws AS (
  SELECT CAST(workspace_id AS STRING) AS workspace_id, workspace_name
  FROM system.access.workspaces_latest
)
  3. p: Live pools with their workspace name.
    Its SQL (6 lines)
    -- Live pools with their workspace name.
p AS (
  SELECT p.*, COALESCE(ws.workspace_name, CAST(p.workspace_id AS STRING), 'Account-level') AS workspace
  FROM pools p LEFT JOIN ws ON ws.workspace_id = CAST(p.workspace_id AS STRING)
  WHERE p.delete_time IS NULL
)

3. Final query

One row per pool.

4. Columns it returns

ColumnMeaning
poolThe pool's name and id.
workspaceThe workspace's name (its id when the name is unknown).
node_typeThe machine type it keeps.
min_idle_instancesMachines kept running with no cluster on them.
idle_autotermination_minutesHow long an extra idle machine stays before it is released.
total_usdDatabricks $ of clusters from the pool (the cloud machine cost is billed by the cloud).
statusCRITICAL, WARN, OK or NOT_ASSESSED (not enough data to judge), from the rule in Rules.
SQL with comments (99 lines)
-- Instance pools: idle instances kept warm. List price; dates from the Time Window.
-- The shared check instance_pools_idle_capacity. Instance pools: per pool, the idle instances it keeps warm and its $.
WITH pools AS (
  SELECT datediff(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), :time_range.min) AS window_days, q.*
  FROM (
  -- List price of every SKU over time, with the date each price really ended.
  WITH price AS (
    SELECT sku_name, cloud, usage_unit, price_start_time, price_end_time,
           CAST(pricing.effective_list.default AS DOUBLE) AS list_rate
    FROM 
  (
      SELECT account_id, sku_name, cloud, currency_code, usage_unit, pricing,
             price_start_time, effective_end AS price_end_time
      FROM (
          SELECT account_id, sku_name, cloud, currency_code, usage_unit, pricing,
                 price_start_time, price_end_time, next_start_time,
                 -- CASE, not LEAST, so a NULL end/next behaves the same on DuckDB and Databricks.
                 CASE
                     WHEN price_end_time IS NULL THEN next_start_time
                     WHEN next_start_time IS NULL THEN price_end_time
                     WHEN price_end_time <= next_start_time THEN price_end_time
                     ELSE next_start_time
                 END AS effective_end
          FROM (
              SELECT account_id, sku_name, cloud, currency_code, usage_unit, pricing,
                     price_start_time, price_end_time,
                     LEAD(price_start_time) OVER (
                         PARTITION BY sku_name, cloud, usage_unit
                         ORDER BY price_start_time, price_end_time NULLS LAST
                     ) AS next_start_time
              FROM `system`.`billing`.`list_prices`
              WHERE currency_code = 'USD'
          ) ranked
      ) capped
      WHERE effective_end IS NULL OR effective_end > price_start_time
  )
   list_prices
  ),
  -- Per pool: DBUs and $ billed in the window.
  cost_rollup AS (
    SELECT u.workspace_id,
           u.usage_metadata.instance_pool_id AS instance_pool_id,
           SUM(u.usage_quantity)                            AS net_dbus,
           SUM(u.usage_quantity * COALESCE(p.list_rate, 0)) AS est_usd_list,
           CASE
             WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN p.list_rate IS NULL AND upper(u.sku_name) NOT LIKE '%FREE_USAGE%'
                           THEN u.usage_quantity ELSE 0 END) > 0 THEN 'unpriced'
             WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN p.list_rate IS NOT NULL THEN u.usage_quantity ELSE 0 END) = 0 THEN 'free'
             ELSE 'priced'
           END                                               AS price_basis
    FROM `system`.`billing`.`usage` u
    LEFT JOIN price p
      ON u.sku_name = p.sku_name AND u.cloud = p.cloud AND u.usage_unit = p.usage_unit
     AND u.usage_end_time >= p.price_start_time
     AND (p.price_end_time IS NULL OR u.usage_end_time < p.price_end_time)
    WHERE upper(u.usage_unit) = 'DBU'
      AND u.usage_metadata.instance_pool_id IS NOT NULL
      AND u.usage_date >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE)
      AND u.usage_date <  date_add(:time_range.max, 1)
    GROUP BY u.workspace_id, u.usage_metadata.instance_pool_id
  )
  SELECT p.instance_pool_id,
         p.instance_pool_name AS instance_pool_name,
         p.node_type, p.min_idle_instances, p.max_capacity,
         p.idle_instance_autotermination_minutes, p.enable_elastic_disk, p.preloaded_spark_version,
         p.preloaded_docker_images, p.tags, p.aws_attributes, p.azure_attributes, p.gcp_attributes, p.disk_spec,
         p.create_time, p.delete_time, p.change_time, p.workspace_id, p.account_id,
         COALESCE(cr.net_dbus, 0)     AS net_dbus,
         COALESCE(cr.est_usd_list, 0) AS est_usd_list,
         COALESCE(cr.price_basis, 'priced') AS price_basis,
         -- status: worst-first band on the standing idle-instance floor (field heuristic; 5 / 20).
         CASE
           WHEN p.min_idle_instances IS NULL THEN 'NOT_ASSESSED'
           WHEN p.min_idle_instances >= 20 THEN 'CRITICAL'
           WHEN p.min_idle_instances >= 5 THEN 'WARN'
           ELSE 'OK'
         END AS status
  FROM (
    SELECT *, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY instance_pool_id ORDER BY change_time DESC) AS rn
    FROM `system`.`compute`.`instance_pools`
  ) p
  LEFT JOIN cost_rollup cr
    ON cr.workspace_id = p.workspace_id
   AND cr.instance_pool_id = p.instance_pool_id
  WHERE p.rn = 1 AND p.delete_time IS NULL
  ORDER BY p.min_idle_instances DESC
  ) q
),
-- Workspace id to name.
ws AS (
  SELECT CAST(workspace_id AS STRING) AS workspace_id, workspace_name
  FROM system.access.workspaces_latest
),
-- Live pools with their workspace name.
p AS (
  SELECT p.*, COALESCE(ws.workspace_name, CAST(p.workspace_id AS STRING), 'Account-level') AS workspace
  FROM pools p LEFT JOIN ws ON ws.workspace_id = CAST(p.workspace_id AS STRING)
  WHERE p.delete_time IS NULL
)
-- Final SELECT: the rows the widgets read.
SELECT concat(instance_pool_name, ' (', instance_pool_id, ')') AS pool, workspace, node_type,
       min_idle_instances, idle_instance_autotermination_minutes AS idle_autotermination_minutes,
       est_usd_list AS total_usd, status
FROM p
ORDER BY min_idle_instances DESC

Failed job runs: $ of runs that failed, per job

Shows: Per job with failed or repaired runs: runs, failures, failure rate, the $ burned and the last error. Used by: The jobs half of Failed runs & queries.

Why it is built this way: The compute of a failed run is paid and delivers nothing; billing per run gives its $.

How its tables join

Grey: system tables. Blue border: a shared check (its steps are listed under Steps). White: steps of the query. Green: the rows the dashboard shows. ◆ marks the columns the joins use.

  1. lakeflow_failed_jobs_wasted_dbus → f: the check's rows
  2. jb → f: LEFT JOIN ON workspace_id, job_id: names
  3. ws → f: LEFT JOIN ON workspace_id: names

1. System tables it reads

  • system.access.workspaces_latest
  • system.billing.list_prices
  • system.billing.usage
  • system.lakeflow.job_run_timeline
  • system.lakeflow.jobs

2. Steps (CTEs), in order

  1. failed_jobs: The shared check lakeflow_failed_jobs_wasted_dbus. Failed job runs: per job, the $ of runs that failed, plus the failed part of runs that were repaired.
    1. price: List price of every SKU over time, with the date each price really ended.
    2. usage_rows: Every billed DBU row of a job in the window, priced at list.
    3. job_usage: Per job: total DBUs and $, and how much billing has no run id.
    4. run_usage: Per job run: DBUs and $.
    5. cand: Job runs that ended in the window.
    6. run_rows: Every time slice of those runs.
    7. slices: Attempts numbered within each run: a repair starts a new attempt.
    8. attempts: One row per attempt: how long it ran and how it ended.
    9. att2: Each run's last attempt number added.
    10. runs: Per run: its final result, total seconds, and seconds in attempts that failed before a repair.
    11. classed: Each run labelled failed, cancelled, repaired (failed, then fixed) or ok, with its $.
    12. last_fail: Each job's most recent failure and its error code.
    13. per_job: Per job: runs, failures, repairs; $ of failed runs plus the failed share of repaired runs.
    14. judged: The job's billing, failure rate and wasted $ added; why one can't be priced.
    Its SQL (243 lines)
    -- Failed job runs: $ of runs that failed, per job. List price; dates from the Time Window.
-- The shared check lakeflow_failed_jobs_wasted_dbus. Failed job runs: per job, the $ of runs that failed, plus the failed part of runs that were repaired.
failed_jobs AS (
  SELECT datediff(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), :time_range.min) AS window_days, q.*
  FROM (
  -- List price of every SKU over time, with the date each price really ended.
  WITH price AS (
    SELECT sku_name, cloud, usage_unit, price_start_time, price_end_time,
           CAST(pricing.effective_list.default AS DOUBLE) AS list_rate
    FROM 
  (
      SELECT account_id, sku_name, cloud, currency_code, usage_unit, pricing,
             price_start_time, effective_end AS price_end_time
      FROM (
          SELECT account_id, sku_name, cloud, currency_code, usage_unit, pricing,
                 price_start_time, price_end_time, next_start_time,
                 -- CASE, not LEAST, so a NULL end/next behaves the same on DuckDB and Databricks.
                 CASE
                     WHEN price_end_time IS NULL THEN next_start_time
                     WHEN next_start_time IS NULL THEN price_end_time
                     WHEN price_end_time <= next_start_time THEN price_end_time
                     ELSE next_start_time
                 END AS effective_end
          FROM (
              SELECT account_id, sku_name, cloud, currency_code, usage_unit, pricing,
                     price_start_time, price_end_time,
                     LEAD(price_start_time) OVER (
                         PARTITION BY sku_name, cloud, usage_unit
                         ORDER BY price_start_time, price_end_time NULLS LAST
                     ) AS next_start_time
              FROM `system`.`billing`.`list_prices`
              WHERE currency_code = 'USD'
          ) ranked
      ) capped
      WHERE effective_end IS NULL OR effective_end > price_start_time
  )
   list_prices
  ),
  -- Every billed DBU row of a job in the window, priced at list.
  usage_rows AS (
    SELECT u.workspace_id,
           u.usage_metadata.job_id     AS job_id,
           u.usage_metadata.job_run_id AS job_run_id,
           u.usage_quantity            AS dbus,
           u.usage_quantity * p.list_rate AS usd,
           CASE WHEN p.list_rate IS NULL AND upper(u.sku_name) NOT LIKE '%FREE_USAGE%'
                THEN u.usage_quantity ELSE 0 END AS unpriced_q,
           CASE WHEN p.list_rate IS NOT NULL THEN u.usage_quantity ELSE 0 END AS priced_q
    FROM `system`.`billing`.`usage` u
    LEFT JOIN price p
      ON  u.sku_name = p.sku_name AND u.cloud = p.cloud AND u.usage_unit = p.usage_unit
      AND u.usage_end_time >= p.price_start_time
      AND (p.price_end_time IS NULL OR u.usage_end_time < p.price_end_time)
    WHERE u.usage_date >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE)
      AND u.usage_date < date_add(:time_range.max, 1)
      AND upper(u.usage_unit) = 'DBU'
      AND u.usage_metadata.job_id IS NOT NULL
  ),
  -- Per job: total DBUs and $, and how much billing has no run id.
  job_usage AS (
    SELECT workspace_id, job_id,
           SUM(dbus) AS net_dbus, SUM(usd) AS usd_priced_part,
           SUM(unpriced_q) AS unpriced_q, SUM(priced_q) AS priced_q,
           SUM(CASE WHEN job_run_id IS NULL THEN dbus ELSE 0 END)       AS unattributed_dbus,
           SUM(CASE WHEN job_run_id IS NULL THEN usd END)               AS unattributed_usd,
           SUM(CASE WHEN job_run_id IS NULL THEN unpriced_q ELSE 0 END) AS unattributed_unpriced_q,
           SUM(CASE WHEN job_run_id IS NOT NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)      AS run_rows
    FROM usage_rows
    GROUP BY workspace_id, job_id
  ),
  -- Per job run: DBUs and $.
  run_usage AS (
    SELECT workspace_id, job_id, job_run_id,
           SUM(dbus) AS run_dbus, SUM(usd) AS run_usd, SUM(unpriced_q) AS run_unpriced_q
    FROM usage_rows
    WHERE job_run_id IS NOT NULL
    GROUP BY workspace_id, job_id, job_run_id
  ),
  -- Job runs that ended in the window.
  cand AS (
    SELECT DISTINCT workspace_id, job_id, run_id
    FROM `system`.`lakeflow`.`job_run_timeline`
    WHERE period_start_time >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE)
      AND period_end_time < CAST(date_add(:time_range.max, 1) AS TIMESTAMP)
      AND result_state IS NOT NULL
  ),
  -- Every time slice of those runs.
  run_rows AS (
    SELECT t.workspace_id, t.job_id, t.run_id, t.period_start_time, t.period_end_time,
           t.result_state, t.termination_code,
           unix_timestamp(t.period_end_time) - unix_timestamp(t.period_start_time) AS slice_s
    FROM `system`.`lakeflow`.`job_run_timeline` t
    JOIN cand c ON c.workspace_id = t.workspace_id AND c.job_id = t.job_id AND c.run_id = t.run_id
  ),
  -- Attempts numbered within each run: a repair starts a new attempt.
  slices AS (
    SELECT r.*,
           1 + COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN r.result_state IS NOT NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) OVER (
                 PARTITION BY r.workspace_id, r.job_id, r.run_id
                 ORDER BY r.period_end_time, r.period_start_time
                 ROWS BETWEEN UNBOUNDED PRECEDING AND 1 PRECEDING), 0) AS attempt_no
    FROM run_rows r
  ),
  -- One row per attempt: how long it ran and how it ended.
  attempts AS (
    SELECT workspace_id, job_id, run_id, attempt_no,
           SUM(slice_s)          AS attempt_s,
           MAX(result_state)     AS attempt_state,   -- one end row per attempt, or none (in flight)
           MAX(termination_code) AS attempt_code,
           MAX(period_end_time)  AS attempt_end
    FROM slices
    GROUP BY workspace_id, job_id, run_id, attempt_no
  ),
  -- Each run's last attempt number added.
  att2 AS (
    SELECT a.*, MAX(a.attempt_no) OVER (PARTITION BY a.workspace_id, a.job_id, a.run_id) AS last_attempt
    FROM attempts a
  ),
  -- Per run: its final result, total seconds, and seconds in attempts that failed before a repair.
  runs AS (
    SELECT workspace_id, job_id, run_id,
           MAX(CASE WHEN attempt_no = last_attempt THEN attempt_state END) AS final_state,
           MAX(CASE WHEN attempt_no = last_attempt THEN attempt_end END)   AS final_end,
           MAX(CASE WHEN attempt_no = last_attempt AND attempt_state IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS in_flight,
           SUM(attempt_s) AS run_s,
           SUM(CASE WHEN attempt_no < last_attempt AND attempt_state IN ('FAILED', 'ERROR', 'TIMED_OUT')
                    THEN attempt_s ELSE 0 END) AS failed_attempt_s
    FROM att2
    GROUP BY workspace_id, job_id, run_id
  ),
  -- Each run labelled failed, cancelled, repaired (failed, then fixed) or ok, with its $.
  classed AS (
    SELECT r.*, ru.run_dbus, ru.run_usd, ru.run_unpriced_q,
           CASE WHEN r.final_state IN ('FAILED', 'ERROR', 'TIMED_OUT') THEN 'failed'
                WHEN r.final_state = 'CANCELLED' THEN 'cancelled'
                WHEN r.failed_attempt_s > 0 THEN 'repaired'
                ELSE 'ok' END AS run_class
    FROM runs r
    LEFT JOIN run_usage ru
      ON ru.workspace_id = r.workspace_id AND ru.job_id = r.job_id AND ru.job_run_id = r.run_id
    WHERE r.in_flight = 0
      AND r.final_end < CAST(date_add(:time_range.max, 1) AS TIMESTAMP)
  ),
  -- Each job's most recent failure and its error code.
  last_fail AS (
    -- the most recent failed attempt (a final failure or a repaired one) of these runs
    SELECT a.workspace_id, a.job_id, a.run_id AS last_failed_run_id,
           a.attempt_code AS last_failed_termination_code
    FROM att2 a
    JOIN classed c ON c.workspace_id = a.workspace_id AND c.job_id = a.job_id AND c.run_id = a.run_id
    WHERE a.attempt_state IN ('FAILED', 'ERROR', 'TIMED_OUT')
    QUALIFY ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY a.workspace_id, a.job_id
                               ORDER BY a.attempt_end DESC, a.run_id DESC) = 1
  ),
  -- Per job: runs, failures, repairs; $ of failed runs plus the failed share of repaired runs.
  per_job AS (
    SELECT workspace_id, job_id,
           COUNT(*) AS distinct_runs,
           SUM(CASE WHEN run_class = 'failed'    THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS failed_runs,
           SUM(CASE WHEN run_class = 'repaired'  THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS repaired_runs,
           SUM(CASE WHEN run_class = 'cancelled' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS cancelled_runs,
           SUM(CASE WHEN run_class = 'failed' AND run_dbus IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS failed_runs_unbilled,
           SUM(CASE WHEN run_class = 'failed' THEN run_dbus
                    WHEN run_class = 'repaired' AND run_s > 0 THEN run_dbus * failed_attempt_s / run_s END) AS wasted_dbus_raw,
           SUM(CASE WHEN run_class = 'failed' THEN run_usd END) AS failed_usd,
           SUM(CASE WHEN run_class = 'repaired' AND run_s > 0 THEN run_usd * failed_attempt_s / run_s END) AS repair_usd,
           SUM(CASE WHEN run_class = 'cancelled' THEN run_usd END) AS cancelled_usd,
           SUM(CASE WHEN run_class IN ('failed', 'repaired') THEN COALESCE(run_unpriced_q, 0) ELSE 0 END) AS waste_unpriced_q
    FROM classed
    GROUP BY workspace_id, job_id
  ),
  -- The job's billing, failure rate and wasted $ added; why one can't be priced.
  judged AS (
    SELECT p.*, j.net_dbus, j.usd_priced_part, j.unpriced_q, j.priced_q, j.unattributed_dbus,
           j.unattributed_usd, j.unattributed_unpriced_q,
           lf.last_failed_run_id, lf.last_failed_termination_code,
           (j.workspace_id IS NOT NULL) AS has_usage,
           p.failed_runs * 100.0 / p.distinct_runs AS rate_raw,
           COALESCE(p.failed_usd, 0) + COALESCE(p.repair_usd, 0) AS waste_raw,
           CASE WHEN j.workspace_id IS NULL THEN 'no_billing_rows'
                WHEN j.run_rows = 0          THEN 'no_run_id_in_billing'
                WHEN p.waste_unpriced_q > 0  THEN 'unpriced'
           END AS na_reason
    FROM per_job p
    LEFT JOIN job_usage j  ON j.workspace_id = p.workspace_id  AND j.job_id = p.job_id
    LEFT JOIN last_fail lf ON lf.workspace_id = p.workspace_id AND lf.job_id = p.job_id
    WHERE p.failed_runs > 0 OR p.repaired_runs > 0
  )
  SELECT workspace_id, job_id,
         distinct_runs, failed_runs, repaired_runs, cancelled_runs, failed_runs_unbilled,
         ROUND(rate_raw, 1)                                                     AS failure_rate_pct,
         last_failed_termination_code, last_failed_run_id,
         CASE WHEN na_reason IN ('no_billing_rows', 'no_run_id_in_billing') THEN NULL
              ELSE ROUND(COALESCE(wasted_dbus_raw, 0), 2) END                   AS wasted_dbus,
         CASE WHEN na_reason IS NULL THEN ROUND(COALESCE(failed_usd, 0), 2) END    AS est_failed_usd_list,
         CASE WHEN na_reason IS NULL THEN ROUND(COALESCE(repair_usd, 0), 2) END    AS est_repair_usd_list,
         CASE WHEN na_reason IS NULL THEN ROUND(waste_raw, 2) END                   AS est_wasted_usd_list,
         CASE WHEN na_reason IS NULL THEN ROUND(COALESCE(cancelled_usd, 0), 2) END AS est_cancelled_usd_list,
         ROUND(net_dbus, 2)                                                     AS net_dbus,
         CASE WHEN NOT has_usage OR unpriced_q > 0 THEN NULL
              ELSE ROUND(COALESCE(usd_priced_part, 0), 2) END                   AS est_usd_list,
         CASE WHEN NOT has_usage THEN NULL
              WHEN unattributed_dbus = 0 THEN 0
              WHEN unattributed_unpriced_q > 0 THEN NULL
              ELSE ROUND(unattributed_usd, 2) END                               AS est_unattributed_usd_list,
         CASE WHEN na_reason IS NULL AND NOT (unpriced_q > 0)
              THEN ROUND(waste_raw * 100.0 / NULLIF(usd_priced_part, 0), 1) END AS wasted_share_pct,
         CASE WHEN NOT has_usage THEN NULL
              WHEN unpriced_q > 0 THEN 'unpriced' WHEN priced_q = 0 THEN 'free' ELSE 'priced' END AS price_basis,
         CASE
           WHEN na_reason = 'no_billing_rows' THEN
             'failed runs or failed attempts found, but no billed usage for this job in the window - the runs may have failed before compute started, or billing has not landed yet'
           WHEN na_reason = 'no_run_id_in_billing' THEN
             'failed runs or failed attempts found, but this job''s billing rows carry no run id, so what they cost cannot be measured (the job''s whole spend is in est_usd_list, not spread over its runs)'
           WHEN na_reason = 'unpriced' THEN
             'the failed runs'' usage has no list price, so there is no dollar figure (wasted_dbus is shown)'
           ELSE
             CONCAT_WS('; ',
               CONCAT(failed_runs, ' of ', distinct_runs, ' runs failed (', CAST(ROUND(rate_raw, 0) AS BIGINT), '%)'),
               CASE WHEN repaired_runs = 1 THEN '1 run failed first and was repaired'
                    WHEN repaired_runs > 1 THEN CONCAT(repaired_runs, ' runs failed first and were repaired') END,
               CASE WHEN failed_runs_unbilled = 1 THEN '1 failed run had no billed usage'
                    WHEN failed_runs_unbilled > 1 THEN CONCAT(failed_runs_unbilled, ' failed runs had no billed usage') END,
               CASE WHEN last_failed_termination_code IS NOT NULL
                    THEN CONCAT('last failure ', last_failed_termination_code) END)
         END                                                                    AS waste_reason,
         na_reason                                                              AS not_assessed_reason,
         -- the $ floor (1) gates only the $ branches, never the failure-rate ones -- a
         -- job failing outright must not read OK just because it is cheap. The rate branches
         -- themselves need 3 runs first -- 1 of 1 failed is not a rate, just one data point.
         CASE
           WHEN na_reason IS NOT NULL                                                        THEN 'NOT_ASSESSED'
           WHEN (distinct_runs >= 3 AND rate_raw >= 50)
             OR waste_raw >= GREATEST(200, 1)                       THEN 'CRITICAL'
           WHEN (distinct_runs >= 3 AND rate_raw >= 10)
             OR waste_raw >= GREATEST(20, 1)                       THEN 'WARN'
           ELSE 'OK'
         END AS status
  FROM judged
  ORDER BY CASE status WHEN 'CRITICAL' THEN 0 WHEN 'WARN' THEN 1 WHEN 'NOT_ASSESSED' THEN 2 ELSE 3 END,
           est_wasted_usd_list DESC NULLS LAST, failed_runs DESC, workspace_id, job_id
  ) q
)
  2. ws: Workspace id to name.
    Its SQL (5 lines)
    -- Workspace id to name.
ws AS (
  SELECT CAST(workspace_id AS STRING) AS workspace_id, workspace_name
  FROM system.access.workspaces_latest
)
  3. jb: Job id to name: the latest row per job.
    Its SQL (6 lines)
    -- Job id to name: the latest row per job.
jb AS (
  SELECT CAST(workspace_id AS STRING) AS workspace_id, CAST(job_id AS STRING) AS job_id, name
  FROM system.lakeflow.jobs
  QUALIFY ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY workspace_id, job_id ORDER BY change_time DESC) = 1
)
  4. f: Workspace and job names added; ranked by wasted $ for the top 10.
    Its SQL (8 lines)
    -- Workspace and job names added; ranked by wasted $ for the top 10.
f AS (
  SELECT f.*, COALESCE(ws.workspace_name, CAST(f.workspace_id AS STRING), 'Account-level') AS workspace, COALESCE(jb.name, CAST(f.job_id AS STRING)) AS job_name,
         ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY f.est_wasted_usd_list DESC NULLS LAST, f.failed_runs DESC, f.job_id) AS rn
  FROM failed_jobs f
  LEFT JOIN ws ON ws.workspace_id = CAST(f.workspace_id AS STRING)
  LEFT JOIN jb ON jb.workspace_id = CAST(f.workspace_id AS STRING) AND jb.job_id = CAST(f.job_id AS STRING)
)

3. Final query

One row per job with failures; the 10 costliest keep their name for the chart.

4. Columns it returns

ColumnMeaning
jobThe job's name and id, as name (id). One-off submitted runs have no name and show the id.
workspaceThe workspace's name (its id when the name is unknown).
statusCRITICAL, WARN, OK or NOT_ASSESSED (not enough data to judge), from the rule in Rules.
top_jobThe job's name for the 10 with the most wasted $; All others for the rest.
runsDistinct runs in the window.
failed_runsRuns that failed.
repaired_runsRuns that were repaired (re-run after a failure).
failure_rate_pctfailed_runs / runs x 100.
wasted_usdList-price $ of the failed runs and the failed attempts of repaired runs.
last_failureThe error code of the latest failed run.
flagged1 when status is CRITICAL or WARN, else 0; tiles add it up to count flagged rows.
SQL with comments (251 lines)
-- Failed job runs: $ of runs that failed, per job. List price; dates from the Time Window.
-- The shared check lakeflow_failed_jobs_wasted_dbus. Failed job runs: per job, the $ of runs that failed, plus the failed part of runs that were repaired.
WITH failed_jobs AS (
  SELECT datediff(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), :time_range.min) AS window_days, q.*
  FROM (
  -- List price of every SKU over time, with the date each price really ended.
  WITH price AS (
    SELECT sku_name, cloud, usage_unit, price_start_time, price_end_time,
           CAST(pricing.effective_list.default AS DOUBLE) AS list_rate
    FROM 
  (
      SELECT account_id, sku_name, cloud, currency_code, usage_unit, pricing,
             price_start_time, effective_end AS price_end_time
      FROM (
          SELECT account_id, sku_name, cloud, currency_code, usage_unit, pricing,
                 price_start_time, price_end_time, next_start_time,
                 -- CASE, not LEAST, so a NULL end/next behaves the same on DuckDB and Databricks.
                 CASE
                     WHEN price_end_time IS NULL THEN next_start_time
                     WHEN next_start_time IS NULL THEN price_end_time
                     WHEN price_end_time <= next_start_time THEN price_end_time
                     ELSE next_start_time
                 END AS effective_end
          FROM (
              SELECT account_id, sku_name, cloud, currency_code, usage_unit, pricing,
                     price_start_time, price_end_time,
                     LEAD(price_start_time) OVER (
                         PARTITION BY sku_name, cloud, usage_unit
                         ORDER BY price_start_time, price_end_time NULLS LAST
                     ) AS next_start_time
              FROM `system`.`billing`.`list_prices`
              WHERE currency_code = 'USD'
          ) ranked
      ) capped
      WHERE effective_end IS NULL OR effective_end > price_start_time
  )
   list_prices
  ),
  -- Every billed DBU row of a job in the window, priced at list.
  usage_rows AS (
    SELECT u.workspace_id,
           u.usage_metadata.job_id     AS job_id,
           u.usage_metadata.job_run_id AS job_run_id,
           u.usage_quantity            AS dbus,
           u.usage_quantity * p.list_rate AS usd,
           CASE WHEN p.list_rate IS NULL AND upper(u.sku_name) NOT LIKE '%FREE_USAGE%'
                THEN u.usage_quantity ELSE 0 END AS unpriced_q,
           CASE WHEN p.list_rate IS NOT NULL THEN u.usage_quantity ELSE 0 END AS priced_q
    FROM `system`.`billing`.`usage` u
    LEFT JOIN price p
      ON  u.sku_name = p.sku_name AND u.cloud = p.cloud AND u.usage_unit = p.usage_unit
      AND u.usage_end_time >= p.price_start_time
      AND (p.price_end_time IS NULL OR u.usage_end_time < p.price_end_time)
    WHERE u.usage_date >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE)
      AND u.usage_date < date_add(:time_range.max, 1)
      AND upper(u.usage_unit) = 'DBU'
      AND u.usage_metadata.job_id IS NOT NULL
  ),
  -- Per job: total DBUs and $, and how much billing has no run id.
  job_usage AS (
    SELECT workspace_id, job_id,
           SUM(dbus) AS net_dbus, SUM(usd) AS usd_priced_part,
           SUM(unpriced_q) AS unpriced_q, SUM(priced_q) AS priced_q,
           SUM(CASE WHEN job_run_id IS NULL THEN dbus ELSE 0 END)       AS unattributed_dbus,
           SUM(CASE WHEN job_run_id IS NULL THEN usd END)               AS unattributed_usd,
           SUM(CASE WHEN job_run_id IS NULL THEN unpriced_q ELSE 0 END) AS unattributed_unpriced_q,
           SUM(CASE WHEN job_run_id IS NOT NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)      AS run_rows
    FROM usage_rows
    GROUP BY workspace_id, job_id
  ),
  -- Per job run: DBUs and $.
  run_usage AS (
    SELECT workspace_id, job_id, job_run_id,
           SUM(dbus) AS run_dbus, SUM(usd) AS run_usd, SUM(unpriced_q) AS run_unpriced_q
    FROM usage_rows
    WHERE job_run_id IS NOT NULL
    GROUP BY workspace_id, job_id, job_run_id
  ),
  -- Job runs that ended in the window.
  cand AS (
    SELECT DISTINCT workspace_id, job_id, run_id
    FROM `system`.`lakeflow`.`job_run_timeline`
    WHERE period_start_time >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE)
      AND period_end_time < CAST(date_add(:time_range.max, 1) AS TIMESTAMP)
      AND result_state IS NOT NULL
  ),
  -- Every time slice of those runs.
  run_rows AS (
    SELECT t.workspace_id, t.job_id, t.run_id, t.period_start_time, t.period_end_time,
           t.result_state, t.termination_code,
           unix_timestamp(t.period_end_time) - unix_timestamp(t.period_start_time) AS slice_s
    FROM `system`.`lakeflow`.`job_run_timeline` t
    JOIN cand c ON c.workspace_id = t.workspace_id AND c.job_id = t.job_id AND c.run_id = t.run_id
  ),
  -- Attempts numbered within each run: a repair starts a new attempt.
  slices AS (
    SELECT r.*,
           1 + COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN r.result_state IS NOT NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) OVER (
                 PARTITION BY r.workspace_id, r.job_id, r.run_id
                 ORDER BY r.period_end_time, r.period_start_time
                 ROWS BETWEEN UNBOUNDED PRECEDING AND 1 PRECEDING), 0) AS attempt_no
    FROM run_rows r
  ),
  -- One row per attempt: how long it ran and how it ended.
  attempts AS (
    SELECT workspace_id, job_id, run_id, attempt_no,
           SUM(slice_s)          AS attempt_s,
           MAX(result_state)     AS attempt_state,   -- one end row per attempt, or none (in flight)
           MAX(termination_code) AS attempt_code,
           MAX(period_end_time)  AS attempt_end
    FROM slices
    GROUP BY workspace_id, job_id, run_id, attempt_no
  ),
  -- Each run's last attempt number added.
  att2 AS (
    SELECT a.*, MAX(a.attempt_no) OVER (PARTITION BY a.workspace_id, a.job_id, a.run_id) AS last_attempt
    FROM attempts a
  ),
  -- Per run: its final result, total seconds, and seconds in attempts that failed before a repair.
  runs AS (
    SELECT workspace_id, job_id, run_id,
           MAX(CASE WHEN attempt_no = last_attempt THEN attempt_state END) AS final_state,
           MAX(CASE WHEN attempt_no = last_attempt THEN attempt_end END)   AS final_end,
           MAX(CASE WHEN attempt_no = last_attempt AND attempt_state IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS in_flight,
           SUM(attempt_s) AS run_s,
           SUM(CASE WHEN attempt_no < last_attempt AND attempt_state IN ('FAILED', 'ERROR', 'TIMED_OUT')
                    THEN attempt_s ELSE 0 END) AS failed_attempt_s
    FROM att2
    GROUP BY workspace_id, job_id, run_id
  ),
  -- Each run labelled failed, cancelled, repaired (failed, then fixed) or ok, with its $.
  classed AS (
    SELECT r.*, ru.run_dbus, ru.run_usd, ru.run_unpriced_q,
           CASE WHEN r.final_state IN ('FAILED', 'ERROR', 'TIMED_OUT') THEN 'failed'
                WHEN r.final_state = 'CANCELLED' THEN 'cancelled'
                WHEN r.failed_attempt_s > 0 THEN 'repaired'
                ELSE 'ok' END AS run_class
    FROM runs r
    LEFT JOIN run_usage ru
      ON ru.workspace_id = r.workspace_id AND ru.job_id = r.job_id AND ru.job_run_id = r.run_id
    WHERE r.in_flight = 0
      AND r.final_end < CAST(date_add(:time_range.max, 1) AS TIMESTAMP)
  ),
  -- Each job's most recent failure and its error code.
  last_fail AS (
    -- the most recent failed attempt (a final failure or a repaired one) of these runs
    SELECT a.workspace_id, a.job_id, a.run_id AS last_failed_run_id,
           a.attempt_code AS last_failed_termination_code
    FROM att2 a
    JOIN classed c ON c.workspace_id = a.workspace_id AND c.job_id = a.job_id AND c.run_id = a.run_id
    WHERE a.attempt_state IN ('FAILED', 'ERROR', 'TIMED_OUT')
    QUALIFY ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY a.workspace_id, a.job_id
                               ORDER BY a.attempt_end DESC, a.run_id DESC) = 1
  ),
  -- Per job: runs, failures, repairs; $ of failed runs plus the failed share of repaired runs.
  per_job AS (
    SELECT workspace_id, job_id,
           COUNT(*) AS distinct_runs,
           SUM(CASE WHEN run_class = 'failed'    THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS failed_runs,
           SUM(CASE WHEN run_class = 'repaired'  THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS repaired_runs,
           SUM(CASE WHEN run_class = 'cancelled' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS cancelled_runs,
           SUM(CASE WHEN run_class = 'failed' AND run_dbus IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS failed_runs_unbilled,
           SUM(CASE WHEN run_class = 'failed' THEN run_dbus
                    WHEN run_class = 'repaired' AND run_s > 0 THEN run_dbus * failed_attempt_s / run_s END) AS wasted_dbus_raw,
           SUM(CASE WHEN run_class = 'failed' THEN run_usd END) AS failed_usd,
           SUM(CASE WHEN run_class = 'repaired' AND run_s > 0 THEN run_usd * failed_attempt_s / run_s END) AS repair_usd,
           SUM(CASE WHEN run_class = 'cancelled' THEN run_usd END) AS cancelled_usd,
           SUM(CASE WHEN run_class IN ('failed', 'repaired') THEN COALESCE(run_unpriced_q, 0) ELSE 0 END) AS waste_unpriced_q
    FROM classed
    GROUP BY workspace_id, job_id
  ),
  -- The job's billing, failure rate and wasted $ added; why one can't be priced.
  judged AS (
    SELECT p.*, j.net_dbus, j.usd_priced_part, j.unpriced_q, j.priced_q, j.unattributed_dbus,
           j.unattributed_usd, j.unattributed_unpriced_q,
           lf.last_failed_run_id, lf.last_failed_termination_code,
           (j.workspace_id IS NOT NULL) AS has_usage,
           p.failed_runs * 100.0 / p.distinct_runs AS rate_raw,
           COALESCE(p.failed_usd, 0) + COALESCE(p.repair_usd, 0) AS waste_raw,
           CASE WHEN j.workspace_id IS NULL THEN 'no_billing_rows'
                WHEN j.run_rows = 0          THEN 'no_run_id_in_billing'
                WHEN p.waste_unpriced_q > 0  THEN 'unpriced'
           END AS na_reason
    FROM per_job p
    LEFT JOIN job_usage j  ON j.workspace_id = p.workspace_id  AND j.job_id = p.job_id
    LEFT JOIN last_fail lf ON lf.workspace_id = p.workspace_id AND lf.job_id = p.job_id
    WHERE p.failed_runs > 0 OR p.repaired_runs > 0
  )
  SELECT workspace_id, job_id,
         distinct_runs, failed_runs, repaired_runs, cancelled_runs, failed_runs_unbilled,
         ROUND(rate_raw, 1)                                                     AS failure_rate_pct,
         last_failed_termination_code, last_failed_run_id,
         CASE WHEN na_reason IN ('no_billing_rows', 'no_run_id_in_billing') THEN NULL
              ELSE ROUND(COALESCE(wasted_dbus_raw, 0), 2) END                   AS wasted_dbus,
         CASE WHEN na_reason IS NULL THEN ROUND(COALESCE(failed_usd, 0), 2) END    AS est_failed_usd_list,
         CASE WHEN na_reason IS NULL THEN ROUND(COALESCE(repair_usd, 0), 2) END    AS est_repair_usd_list,
         CASE WHEN na_reason IS NULL THEN ROUND(waste_raw, 2) END                   AS est_wasted_usd_list,
         CASE WHEN na_reason IS NULL THEN ROUND(COALESCE(cancelled_usd, 0), 2) END AS est_cancelled_usd_list,
         ROUND(net_dbus, 2)                                                     AS net_dbus,
         CASE WHEN NOT has_usage OR unpriced_q > 0 THEN NULL
              ELSE ROUND(COALESCE(usd_priced_part, 0), 2) END                   AS est_usd_list,
         CASE WHEN NOT has_usage THEN NULL
              WHEN unattributed_dbus = 0 THEN 0
              WHEN unattributed_unpriced_q > 0 THEN NULL
              ELSE ROUND(unattributed_usd, 2) END                               AS est_unattributed_usd_list,
         CASE WHEN na_reason IS NULL AND NOT (unpriced_q > 0)
              THEN ROUND(waste_raw * 100.0 / NULLIF(usd_priced_part, 0), 1) END AS wasted_share_pct,
         CASE WHEN NOT has_usage THEN NULL
              WHEN unpriced_q > 0 THEN 'unpriced' WHEN priced_q = 0 THEN 'free' ELSE 'priced' END AS price_basis,
         CASE
           WHEN na_reason = 'no_billing_rows' THEN
             'failed runs or failed attempts found, but no billed usage for this job in the window - the runs may have failed before compute started, or billing has not landed yet'
           WHEN na_reason = 'no_run_id_in_billing' THEN
             'failed runs or failed attempts found, but this job''s billing rows carry no run id, so what they cost cannot be measured (the job''s whole spend is in est_usd_list, not spread over its runs)'
           WHEN na_reason = 'unpriced' THEN
             'the failed runs'' usage has no list price, so there is no dollar figure (wasted_dbus is shown)'
