  2. ws: Workspace id to name.
    Its SQL (5 lines)
    -- Workspace id to name.
ws AS (
  SELECT CAST(workspace_id AS STRING) AS workspace_id, workspace_name
  FROM system.access.workspaces_latest
)
  3. d: Workspace name added.
    Its SQL (5 lines)
    -- Workspace name added.
d AS (
  SELECT d.*, COALESCE(ws.workspace_name, CAST(d.workspace_id AS STRING), 'Account-level') AS workspace
  FROM dormant d LEFT JOIN ws ON ws.workspace_id = CAST(d.workspace_id AS STRING)
)

3. Final query

One row per endpoint, worst first.

4. Columns it returns

ColumnMeaning
endpointThe endpoint's name and id.
workspaceThe workspace's name (its id when the name is unknown).
statusCRITICAL, WARN, OK or NOT_ASSESSED (not enough data to judge), from the rule in Rules.
requestsRequests in the history kept (90 days).
last_requestThe day of its last request.
billed_dbusDBUs billed.
trackingon or off.
not_assessed_reasonWhy it wasn't judged (for example tracking off).
SQL with comments (107 lines)
-- Dormant serving endpoints: no traffic. List price; dates from the Time Window.
-- The shared check compute_serving_dormant_endpoints. Dormant endpoints: per serving endpoint, requests and when it last had one.
WITH dormant AS (
  SELECT datediff(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), :time_range.min) AS window_days, q.*
  FROM (
  -- Each endpoint's served-entity history: what it serves and when that last changed.
  WITH history AS (
    SELECT se.*,
           MAX(se.endpoint_config_version) OVER (PARTITION BY se.workspace_id, se.endpoint_id) AS current_version,
           MAX(se.endpoint_delete_time) OVER (PARTITION BY se.workspace_id, se.endpoint_id) AS deleted_time,
           MAX(se.change_time) OVER (PARTITION BY se.workspace_id, se.endpoint_id) AS latest_change_time,
           ROW_NUMBER() OVER (
             PARTITION BY se.workspace_id, se.endpoint_id, se.served_entity_id ORDER BY se.change_time DESC
           ) AS _rn
    FROM `system`.`serving`.`served_entities` se
  ),
  -- Every entity the endpoint ever served, so traffic to an old version still counts.
  entity_map AS (
    -- every entity the endpoint ever served, so traffic to a retired version still counts
    SELECT DISTINCT workspace_id, endpoint_id, served_entity_id FROM history
  ),
  -- Requests per served entity in the window, and whether request tracking is on.
  traffic AS (
    SELECT m.workspace_id, m.endpoint_id,
           SUM(CASE WHEN eu.request_time >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE)
                     AND eu.request_time < date_add(:time_range.max, 1) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS total_requests,
           MAX(CASE WHEN eu.request_time < date_add(:time_range.max, 1) THEN CAST(eu.request_time AS DATE) END) AS last_request_date,
           -- any tracked request in the table's retention shows usage tracking is on
           COUNT(*) AS tracked_requests_ever
    FROM entity_map m
    JOIN `system`.`serving`.`endpoint_usage` eu
      ON eu.workspace_id = m.workspace_id AND eu.served_entity_id = m.served_entity_id
    GROUP BY m.workspace_id, m.endpoint_id
  ),
  -- DBUs billed per endpoint in the window.
  billed AS (
    SELECT workspace_id, usage_metadata.endpoint_id AS endpoint_id, SUM(usage_quantity) AS billed_dbus
    FROM `system`.`billing`.`usage`
    WHERE usage_metadata.endpoint_id IS NOT NULL
      AND upper(usage_unit) = 'DBU'
      AND usage_date >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE)
      AND usage_date < date_add(:time_range.max, 1)
    GROUP BY workspace_id, usage_metadata.endpoint_id
  ),
  -- One row per endpoint: requests, last request and billing for its current entities.
  per_endpoint AS (
    -- one row per endpoint; only the entities in its current config are counted
    SELECT workspace_id, endpoint_id,
           MAX(endpoint_name) AS endpoint_name,
           COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN endpoint_config_version = current_version OR current_version IS NULL
                               THEN served_entity_id END) AS served_entity_count,
           MAX(latest_change_time) AS latest_change_time,
           MAX(deleted_time) AS endpoint_delete_time,
           MIN(CASE WHEN entity_type = 'FOUNDATION_MODEL' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS all_foundation
    FROM history
    WHERE _rn = 1
    GROUP BY workspace_id, endpoint_id
  ),
  -- Status: no requests at all is Critical; tracking off or a recent change is not judged.
  judged AS (
    SELECT pe.*,
           COALESCE(t.total_requests, 0) AS total_requests,
           t.last_request_date,
           COALESCE(t.tracked_requests_ever, 0) > 0 AS usage_tracked,
           COALESCE(b.billed_dbus, 0) AS billed_dbus
    FROM per_endpoint pe
    LEFT JOIN traffic t ON t.workspace_id = pe.workspace_id AND t.endpoint_id = pe.endpoint_id
    LEFT JOIN billed b ON b.workspace_id = pe.workspace_id AND b.endpoint_id = pe.endpoint_id
  )
  SELECT
    workspace_id,
    endpoint_id,
    endpoint_name,
    served_entity_count,
    latest_change_time,
    CAST(latest_change_time AS DATE) AS latest_change_date,
    total_requests,
    last_request_date,
    usage_tracked,
    ROUND(billed_dbus, 2) AS billed_dbus,
    CASE
      WHEN total_requests >= 10 THEN 'OK'
      WHEN NOT usage_tracked THEN 'NOT_ASSESSED'
      WHEN latest_change_time >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE) THEN 'NOT_ASSESSED'
      WHEN billed_dbus = 0 THEN 'OK'   -- idle, but it costs nothing while idle
      WHEN total_requests = 0 THEN 'CRITICAL'
      ELSE 'WARN'
    END AS status,
    CASE
      WHEN total_requests >= 10 THEN NULL
      WHEN NOT usage_tracked THEN 'usage_tracking_off'
      WHEN latest_change_time >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE) THEN 'changed_in_window'
    END AS not_assessed_reason
  FROM judged
  WHERE endpoint_delete_time IS NULL
    AND NOT (endpoint_name LIKE 'databricks-%' AND all_foundation = 1)
  ORDER BY
    CASE status WHEN 'CRITICAL' THEN 0 WHEN 'WARN' THEN 1 WHEN 'NOT_ASSESSED' THEN 2 ELSE 3 END,
    billed_dbus DESC, total_requests ASC
  ) q
),
-- Workspace id to name.
ws AS (
  SELECT CAST(workspace_id AS STRING) AS workspace_id, workspace_name
  FROM system.access.workspaces_latest
),
-- Workspace name added.
d AS (
  SELECT d.*, COALESCE(ws.workspace_name, CAST(d.workspace_id AS STRING), 'Account-level') AS workspace
  FROM dormant d LEFT JOIN ws ON ws.workspace_id = CAST(d.workspace_id AS STRING)
)
-- Final SELECT: the rows the widgets read.
SELECT concat(COALESCE(endpoint_name, endpoint_id), ' (', endpoint_id, ')') AS endpoint, workspace, status,
       total_requests AS requests, last_request_date AS last_request, billed_dbus,
       CASE WHEN usage_tracked THEN 'on' ELSE 'off' END AS tracking, not_assessed_reason
FROM d
ORDER BY CASE status WHEN 'CRITICAL' THEN 0 WHEN 'WARN' THEN 1 ELSE 2 END, billed_dbus DESC NULLS LAST

Stale jobs: no run for a long time

Shows: Jobs with no recent run: Warn after 30 days, Critical after 90. Used by: The stale jobs table on Flagged, not priced.

Why it is built this way: A job that hasn't run for months is clutter at best, and may still hold clusters or permissions.

How its tables join

Grey: system tables. Blue border: a shared check (its steps are listed under Steps). White: steps of the query. Green: the rows the dashboard shows. ◆ marks the columns the joins use.

  1. lakeflow_stale_zombie_jobs → s: the check's rows
  2. ws → s: LEFT JOIN ON workspace_id: names

1. System tables it reads

  • system.access.workspaces_latest
  • system.billing.list_prices
  • system.billing.usage
  • system.lakeflow.job_run_timeline
  • system.lakeflow.jobs

2. Steps (CTEs), in order

  1. stale: The shared check lakeflow_stale_zombie_jobs. Stale jobs: per job, its last run; Warn after 30 days without one, Critical after 90.
    1. latest_jobs: Each job's latest definition, deleted ones left out.
    2. last_run: Each job's most recent run start.
    3. capture_floor: How far back the workspace's run history goes, so a job isn't called stale too early.
    4. price: List price of every SKU over time, with the date each price really ended.
    5. cost_rollup: Per job: DBUs and $ billed in the window.
    Its SQL (119 lines)
    -- Stale jobs: no run for a long time. List price; dates from the Time Window.
-- The shared check lakeflow_stale_zombie_jobs. Stale jobs: per job, its last run; Warn after 30 days without one, Critical after 90.
stale AS (
  SELECT datediff(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), :time_range.min) AS window_days, q.*
  FROM (
  -- Each job's latest definition, deleted ones left out.
  WITH latest_jobs AS (
    SELECT workspace_id, job_id, name, run_as, creator_id, create_time, delete_time
    FROM `system`.`lakeflow`.`jobs`
    QUALIFY ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY workspace_id, job_id ORDER BY change_time DESC) = 1
  ),
  -- Each job's most recent run start.
  last_run AS (
    SELECT workspace_id, job_id, MAX(period_start_time) AS last_run_start
    FROM `system`.`lakeflow`.`job_run_timeline`
    GROUP BY workspace_id, job_id
  ),
  -- How far back the workspace's run history goes, so a job isn't called stale too early.
  capture_floor AS (
    -- how far back this WORKSPACE's own job_run_timeline history reaches, unfiltered by job
    SELECT workspace_id,
           datediff(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), MIN(period_start_time)) AS capture_floor_days
    FROM `system`.`lakeflow`.`job_run_timeline`
    GROUP BY workspace_id
  ),
  -- List price of every SKU over time, with the date each price really ended.
  price AS (
    SELECT sku_name, cloud, usage_unit, price_start_time, price_end_time,
           CAST(pricing.effective_list.default AS DOUBLE) AS list_rate
    FROM 
  (
      SELECT account_id, sku_name, cloud, currency_code, usage_unit, pricing,
             price_start_time, effective_end AS price_end_time
      FROM (
          SELECT account_id, sku_name, cloud, currency_code, usage_unit, pricing,
                 price_start_time, price_end_time, next_start_time,
                 -- CASE, not LEAST, so a NULL end/next behaves the same on DuckDB and Databricks.
                 CASE
                     WHEN price_end_time IS NULL THEN next_start_time
                     WHEN next_start_time IS NULL THEN price_end_time
                     WHEN price_end_time <= next_start_time THEN price_end_time
                     ELSE next_start_time
                 END AS effective_end
          FROM (
              SELECT account_id, sku_name, cloud, currency_code, usage_unit, pricing,
                     price_start_time, price_end_time,
                     LEAD(price_start_time) OVER (
                         PARTITION BY sku_name, cloud, usage_unit
                         ORDER BY price_start_time, price_end_time NULLS LAST
                     ) AS next_start_time
              FROM `system`.`billing`.`list_prices`
              WHERE currency_code = 'USD'
          ) ranked
      ) capped
      WHERE effective_end IS NULL OR effective_end > price_start_time
  )
   list_prices
  ),
  -- Per job: DBUs and $ billed in the window.
  cost_rollup AS (
    SELECT u.workspace_id,
           u.usage_metadata.job_id AS job_id,
           SUM(u.usage_quantity)                            AS net_dbus,
           SUM(u.usage_quantity * COALESCE(p.list_rate, 0)) AS est_usd_list,
           CASE
             WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN p.list_rate IS NULL AND upper(u.sku_name) NOT LIKE '%FREE_USAGE%'
                           THEN u.usage_quantity ELSE 0 END) > 0 THEN 'unpriced'
             WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN p.list_rate IS NOT NULL THEN u.usage_quantity ELSE 0 END) = 0 THEN 'free'
             ELSE 'priced'
           END                                               AS price_basis
    FROM `system`.`billing`.`usage` u
    LEFT JOIN price p
      ON u.sku_name = p.sku_name AND u.cloud = p.cloud AND u.usage_unit = p.usage_unit
     AND u.usage_end_time >= p.price_start_time
     AND (p.price_end_time IS NULL OR u.usage_end_time < p.price_end_time)
    WHERE upper(u.usage_unit) = 'DBU'
      AND u.usage_metadata.job_id IS NOT NULL
      AND u.usage_date >= dateadd(day, -30, current_date())
      AND u.usage_date <  date_add(:time_range.max, 1)
    GROUP BY u.workspace_id, u.usage_metadata.job_id
  )
  SELECT j.workspace_id, j.job_id,
         j.name AS name,
         r.last_run_start,
         j.create_time,
         COALESCE(cf.capture_floor_days, 0) AS capture_floor_days,
         CASE WHEN r.last_run_start IS NULL
                OR r.last_run_start < dateadd(day, -30, current_date())
              THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS is_stale,
         COALESCE(cr.net_dbus, 0)     AS net_dbus,
         COALESCE(cr.est_usd_list, 0) AS est_usd_list,
         COALESCE(cr.price_basis, 'priced') AS price_basis,
         -- status: worst-first band on days since last run (field heuristic; 30 / 90).
         -- a NULL last_run_start is NOT_ASSESSED, never WARN/CRITICAL, while the job or this workspace's
         -- own capture history is too young to call it stale (too_young_to_call_stale).
         CASE
           WHEN r.last_run_start IS NULL
                AND (COALESCE(cf.capture_floor_days, 0) < 30
                     OR j.create_time > dateadd(day, -30, current_date()))
                THEN 'NOT_ASSESSED'
           WHEN r.last_run_start IS NULL THEN 'WARN'   -- confirmed no run in a long-enough capture window
           WHEN r.last_run_start < dateadd(day, -90, current_date()) THEN 'CRITICAL'
           WHEN r.last_run_start < dateadd(day, -30, current_date()) THEN 'WARN'
           ELSE 'OK'
         END AS status,
         CASE
           WHEN r.last_run_start IS NULL
                AND (COALESCE(cf.capture_floor_days, 0) < 30
                     OR j.create_time > dateadd(day, -30, current_date()))
                THEN 'too_young_to_call_stale'
         END AS not_assessed_reason
  FROM latest_jobs j
  LEFT JOIN last_run r       ON j.workspace_id = r.workspace_id AND j.job_id = r.job_id
  LEFT JOIN capture_floor cf ON j.workspace_id = cf.workspace_id
  LEFT JOIN cost_rollup cr   ON j.workspace_id = cr.workspace_id AND j.job_id = cr.job_id
  WHERE j.delete_time IS NULL
  ORDER BY last_run_start ASC
  ) q
)
  2. ws: Workspace id to name.
    Its SQL (5 lines)
    -- Workspace id to name.
ws AS (
  SELECT CAST(workspace_id AS STRING) AS workspace_id, workspace_name
  FROM system.access.workspaces_latest
)
  3. s: Workspace name added.
    Its SQL (5 lines)
    -- Workspace name added.
s AS (
  SELECT s.*, COALESCE(ws.workspace_name, CAST(s.workspace_id AS STRING), 'Account-level') AS workspace
  FROM stale s LEFT JOIN ws ON ws.workspace_id = CAST(s.workspace_id AS STRING)
)

3. Final query

One row per job, worst first.

4. Columns it returns

ColumnMeaning
jobThe job's name and id, as name (id). One-off submitted runs have no name and show the id.
workspaceThe workspace's name (its id when the name is unknown).
statusCRITICAL, WARN, OK or NOT_ASSESSED (not enough data to judge), from the rule in Rules.
last_runThe day of its last run.
createdThe day it was created.
total_usdIts $ in the window.
flagged1 when status is CRITICAL or WARN, else 0; tiles add it up to count flagged rows.
SQL with comments (126 lines)
-- Stale jobs: no run for a long time. List price; dates from the Time Window.
-- The shared check lakeflow_stale_zombie_jobs. Stale jobs: per job, its last run; Warn after 30 days without one, Critical after 90.
WITH stale AS (
  SELECT datediff(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), :time_range.min) AS window_days, q.*
  FROM (
  -- Each job's latest definition, deleted ones left out.
  WITH latest_jobs AS (
    SELECT workspace_id, job_id, name, run_as, creator_id, create_time, delete_time
    FROM `system`.`lakeflow`.`jobs`
    QUALIFY ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY workspace_id, job_id ORDER BY change_time DESC) = 1
  ),
  -- Each job's most recent run start.
  last_run AS (
    SELECT workspace_id, job_id, MAX(period_start_time) AS last_run_start
    FROM `system`.`lakeflow`.`job_run_timeline`
    GROUP BY workspace_id, job_id
  ),
  -- How far back the workspace's run history goes, so a job isn't called stale too early.
  capture_floor AS (
    -- how far back this WORKSPACE's own job_run_timeline history reaches, unfiltered by job
    SELECT workspace_id,
           datediff(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), MIN(period_start_time)) AS capture_floor_days
    FROM `system`.`lakeflow`.`job_run_timeline`
    GROUP BY workspace_id
  ),
  -- List price of every SKU over time, with the date each price really ended.
  price AS (
    SELECT sku_name, cloud, usage_unit, price_start_time, price_end_time,
           CAST(pricing.effective_list.default AS DOUBLE) AS list_rate
    FROM 
  (
      SELECT account_id, sku_name, cloud, currency_code, usage_unit, pricing,
             price_start_time, effective_end AS price_end_time
      FROM (
          SELECT account_id, sku_name, cloud, currency_code, usage_unit, pricing,
                 price_start_time, price_end_time, next_start_time,
                 -- CASE, not LEAST, so a NULL end/next behaves the same on DuckDB and Databricks.
                 CASE
                     WHEN price_end_time IS NULL THEN next_start_time
                     WHEN next_start_time IS NULL THEN price_end_time
                     WHEN price_end_time <= next_start_time THEN price_end_time
                     ELSE next_start_time
                 END AS effective_end
          FROM (
              SELECT account_id, sku_name, cloud, currency_code, usage_unit, pricing,
                     price_start_time, price_end_time,
                     LEAD(price_start_time) OVER (
                         PARTITION BY sku_name, cloud, usage_unit
                         ORDER BY price_start_time, price_end_time NULLS LAST
                     ) AS next_start_time
              FROM `system`.`billing`.`list_prices`
              WHERE currency_code = 'USD'
          ) ranked
      ) capped
      WHERE effective_end IS NULL OR effective_end > price_start_time
  )
   list_prices
  ),
  -- Per job: DBUs and $ billed in the window.
  cost_rollup AS (
    SELECT u.workspace_id,
           u.usage_metadata.job_id AS job_id,
           SUM(u.usage_quantity)                            AS net_dbus,
           SUM(u.usage_quantity * COALESCE(p.list_rate, 0)) AS est_usd_list,
           CASE
             WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN p.list_rate IS NULL AND upper(u.sku_name) NOT LIKE '%FREE_USAGE%'
                           THEN u.usage_quantity ELSE 0 END) > 0 THEN 'unpriced'
             WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN p.list_rate IS NOT NULL THEN u.usage_quantity ELSE 0 END) = 0 THEN 'free'
             ELSE 'priced'
           END                                               AS price_basis
    FROM `system`.`billing`.`usage` u
    LEFT JOIN price p
      ON u.sku_name = p.sku_name AND u.cloud = p.cloud AND u.usage_unit = p.usage_unit
     AND u.usage_end_time >= p.price_start_time
     AND (p.price_end_time IS NULL OR u.usage_end_time < p.price_end_time)
    WHERE upper(u.usage_unit) = 'DBU'
      AND u.usage_metadata.job_id IS NOT NULL
      AND u.usage_date >= dateadd(day, -30, current_date())
      AND u.usage_date <  date_add(:time_range.max, 1)
    GROUP BY u.workspace_id, u.usage_metadata.job_id
  )
  SELECT j.workspace_id, j.job_id,
         j.name AS name,
         r.last_run_start,
         j.create_time,
         COALESCE(cf.capture_floor_days, 0) AS capture_floor_days,
         CASE WHEN r.last_run_start IS NULL
                OR r.last_run_start < dateadd(day, -30, current_date())
              THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS is_stale,
         COALESCE(cr.net_dbus, 0)     AS net_dbus,
         COALESCE(cr.est_usd_list, 0) AS est_usd_list,
         COALESCE(cr.price_basis, 'priced') AS price_basis,
         -- status: worst-first band on days since last run (field heuristic; 30 / 90).
         -- a NULL last_run_start is NOT_ASSESSED, never WARN/CRITICAL, while the job or this workspace's
         -- own capture history is too young to call it stale (too_young_to_call_stale).
         CASE
           WHEN r.last_run_start IS NULL
                AND (COALESCE(cf.capture_floor_days, 0) < 30
                     OR j.create_time > dateadd(day, -30, current_date()))
                THEN 'NOT_ASSESSED'
           WHEN r.last_run_start IS NULL THEN 'WARN'   -- confirmed no run in a long-enough capture window
           WHEN r.last_run_start < dateadd(day, -90, current_date()) THEN 'CRITICAL'
           WHEN r.last_run_start < dateadd(day, -30, current_date()) THEN 'WARN'
           ELSE 'OK'
         END AS status,
         CASE
           WHEN r.last_run_start IS NULL
                AND (COALESCE(cf.capture_floor_days, 0) < 30
                     OR j.create_time > dateadd(day, -30, current_date()))
                THEN 'too_young_to_call_stale'
         END AS not_assessed_reason
  FROM latest_jobs j
  LEFT JOIN last_run r       ON j.workspace_id = r.workspace_id AND j.job_id = r.job_id
  LEFT JOIN capture_floor cf ON j.workspace_id = cf.workspace_id
  LEFT JOIN cost_rollup cr   ON j.workspace_id = cr.workspace_id AND j.job_id = cr.job_id
  WHERE j.delete_time IS NULL
  ORDER BY last_run_start ASC
  ) q
),
-- Workspace id to name.
ws AS (
  SELECT CAST(workspace_id AS STRING) AS workspace_id, workspace_name
  FROM system.access.workspaces_latest
),
-- Workspace name added.
s AS (
  SELECT s.*, COALESCE(ws.workspace_name, CAST(s.workspace_id AS STRING), 'Account-level') AS workspace
  FROM stale s LEFT JOIN ws ON ws.workspace_id = CAST(s.workspace_id AS STRING)
)
-- Final SELECT: the rows the widgets read.
SELECT concat(COALESCE(name, CAST(job_id AS STRING)), ' (', job_id, ')') AS job, workspace, status,
       CAST(CAST(last_run_start AS DATE) AS STRING) AS last_run, CAST(CAST(create_time AS DATE) AS STRING) AS created,
       est_usd_list AS total_usd, CASE WHEN status IN ('CRITICAL', 'WARN') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS flagged
FROM s
ORDER BY CASE status WHEN 'CRITICAL' THEN 0 WHEN 'WARN' THEN 1 ELSE 2 END, last_run NULLS FIRST

Jobs on all-purpose clusters

Shows: Jobs whose tasks ran on all-purpose clusters (interactive pricing) instead of job clusters. Used by: The jobs-on-all-purpose table on Flagged, not priced.

Why it is built this way: Scheduled work on an all-purpose cluster pays the all-purpose rate, higher per DBU than a job cluster's.

How its tables join

Grey: system tables. Blue border: a shared check (its steps are listed under Steps). White: steps of the query. Green: the rows the dashboard shows. ◆ marks the columns the joins use.

  1. lakeflow_jobs_on_all_purpose → a: the check's rows
  2. cl → a: LEFT JOIN ON cluster_id: names
  3. jb → a: LEFT JOIN ON workspace_id, job_id: names
  4. ws → a: LEFT JOIN ON workspace_id: names

1. System tables it reads

  • system.access.workspaces_latest
  • system.billing.list_prices
  • system.billing.usage
  • system.compute.clusters
  • system.lakeflow.job_task_run_timeline
  • system.lakeflow.jobs

2. Steps (CTEs), in order

  1. ap: The shared check lakeflow_jobs_on_all_purpose. Jobs on all-purpose clusters: per job and cluster, task runs and the job's own $.
    1. task_compute: Every task run in the window and each cluster it ran on.
    2. serverless_runs: Runs that ran on serverless.
    3. dropped: Per workspace: runs with no compute recorded and not serverless (can't be judged).
    4. warehouse_compute: Compute ids that are SQL warehouses, not clusters.
    5. cluster_jobs: Per cluster: how many different jobs ran on it.
    6. price: List price of every SKU over time, with the date each price really ended.
    7. cost_rollup: Per cluster and job: the job's own billed DBUs and $ on that cluster.
    8. task_hours: The hours each job had a task running on each cluster.
    9. cluster_hours: Per cluster and hour: DBUs and $ billed.
    10. hour_cost: When billing has no job id: the cluster's $ in the hours the job ran on it.
    11. finding: Per job and cluster: the cluster's source (UI, API, JOB, ...), task runs and $.
    Its SQL (230 lines)
    -- Jobs on all-purpose clusters. List price; dates from the Time Window.
-- The shared check lakeflow_jobs_on_all_purpose. Jobs on all-purpose clusters: per job and cluster, task runs and the job's own $.
ap AS (
  SELECT datediff(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), :time_range.min) AS window_days, q.*
  FROM (
  -- Every task run in the window and each cluster it ran on.
  WITH task_compute AS (
    SELECT workspace_id, job_id, run_id, task_key, EXPLODE(compute_ids) AS compute_id
    FROM `system`.`lakeflow`.`job_task_run_timeline`
    WHERE period_start_time >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE)
      AND period_end_time < CAST(date_add(:time_range.max, 1) AS TIMESTAMP)
      AND result_state IS NOT NULL
      AND compute_ids IS NOT NULL
      AND size(compute_ids) > 0
  ),
  -- Runs that ran on serverless.
  serverless_runs AS (
    SELECT DISTINCT workspace_id, run_id
    FROM (
      SELECT workspace_id, run_id, EXPLODE(compute) AS c
      FROM `system`.`lakeflow`.`job_task_run_timeline`
      WHERE period_start_time >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE)
        AND period_end_time < CAST(date_add(:time_range.max, 1) AS TIMESTAMP)
        AND result_state IS NOT NULL
        AND compute IS NOT NULL
        AND size(compute) > 0
    ) exploded
    WHERE upper(c.type) = 'SERVERLESS'
  ),
  -- Per workspace: runs with no compute recorded and not serverless (can't be judged).
  dropped AS (
    SELECT t.workspace_id, COUNT(DISTINCT t.run_id) AS dropped_runs
    FROM `system`.`lakeflow`.`job_task_run_timeline` t
    LEFT JOIN serverless_runs s ON s.workspace_id = t.workspace_id AND s.run_id = t.run_id
    WHERE t.period_start_time >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE)
      AND t.period_end_time < CAST(date_add(:time_range.max, 1) AS TIMESTAMP)
      AND t.result_state IS NOT NULL
      AND (t.compute_ids IS NULL OR size(t.compute_ids) = 0)
      AND s.run_id IS NULL
    GROUP BY t.workspace_id
  ),
  -- Compute ids that are SQL warehouses, not clusters.
  warehouse_compute AS (
    SELECT DISTINCT workspace_id, w.warehouse_id AS compute_id
    FROM (
      SELECT workspace_id, EXPLODE(compute) AS w
      FROM `system`.`lakeflow`.`job_task_run_timeline`
      WHERE period_start_time >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE)
        AND period_end_time < CAST(date_add(:time_range.max, 1) AS TIMESTAMP)
        AND result_state IS NOT NULL
        AND compute IS NOT NULL
        AND size(compute) > 0
    ) exploded
    WHERE w.warehouse_id IS NOT NULL
  ),
  -- Per cluster: how many different jobs ran on it.
  cluster_jobs AS (
    SELECT workspace_id, compute_id, COUNT(DISTINCT job_id) AS jobs_sharing_cluster
    FROM task_compute
    GROUP BY workspace_id, compute_id
  ),
  -- List price of every SKU over time, with the date each price really ended.
  price AS (
    SELECT sku_name, cloud, usage_unit, price_start_time, price_end_time,
           CAST(pricing.effective_list.default AS DOUBLE) AS list_rate
    FROM 
  (
      SELECT account_id, sku_name, cloud, currency_code, usage_unit, pricing,
             price_start_time, effective_end AS price_end_time
      FROM (
          SELECT account_id, sku_name, cloud, currency_code, usage_unit, pricing,
                 price_start_time, price_end_time, next_start_time,
                 -- CASE, not LEAST, so a NULL end/next behaves the same on DuckDB and Databricks.
                 CASE
                     WHEN price_end_time IS NULL THEN next_start_time
                     WHEN next_start_time IS NULL THEN price_end_time
                     WHEN price_end_time <= next_start_time THEN price_end_time
                     ELSE next_start_time
                 END AS effective_end
          FROM (
              SELECT account_id, sku_name, cloud, currency_code, usage_unit, pricing,
                     price_start_time, price_end_time,
                     LEAD(price_start_time) OVER (
                         PARTITION BY sku_name, cloud, usage_unit
                         ORDER BY price_start_time, price_end_time NULLS LAST
                     ) AS next_start_time
              FROM `system`.`billing`.`list_prices`
              WHERE currency_code = 'USD'
          ) ranked
      ) capped
      WHERE effective_end IS NULL OR effective_end > price_start_time
  )
   list_prices
  ),
  -- Per cluster and job: the job's own billed DBUs and $ on that cluster.
  cost_rollup AS (
    -- Pre-aggregated per (cluster, job) over the SAME window as the finding, from usage_metadata.
    -- job_id: this job's OWN billed usage on the cluster, not the cluster's whole bill - notebook
    -- or another job's usage on the same shared cluster carries a different (or no) job_id and is
    -- excluded here, never folded into this job's row.
    SELECT u.workspace_id,
           u.usage_metadata.cluster_id                      AS cluster_id,
           u.usage_metadata.job_id                           AS job_id,
           SUM(u.usage_quantity)                            AS net_dbus,
           SUM(u.usage_quantity * COALESCE(p.list_rate, 0)) AS est_usd_list,
           CASE
             WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN p.list_rate IS NULL AND upper(u.sku_name) NOT LIKE '%FREE_USAGE%'
                           THEN u.usage_quantity ELSE 0 END) > 0 THEN 'unpriced'
             WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN p.list_rate IS NOT NULL THEN u.usage_quantity ELSE 0 END) = 0 THEN 'free'
             ELSE 'priced'
           END                                               AS price_basis
    FROM `system`.`billing`.`usage` u
    LEFT JOIN price p
      ON u.sku_name = p.sku_name AND u.cloud = p.cloud AND u.usage_unit = p.usage_unit
     AND u.usage_end_time >= p.price_start_time
     AND (p.price_end_time IS NULL OR u.usage_end_time < p.price_end_time)
    WHERE upper(u.usage_unit) = 'DBU'
      AND u.usage_metadata.cluster_id IS NOT NULL
      AND u.usage_metadata.job_id IS NOT NULL
      AND u.usage_date >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE)
      AND u.usage_date <  date_add(:time_range.max, 1)
    GROUP BY u.workspace_id, u.usage_metadata.cluster_id, u.usage_metadata.job_id
  ),
  -- The hours each job had a task running on each cluster.
  task_hours AS (
    SELECT DISTINCT workspace_id, job_id, compute_id, date_trunc('HOUR', period_start_time) AS usage_hour
    FROM (
      SELECT workspace_id, job_id, period_start_time, EXPLODE(compute_ids) AS compute_id
      FROM `system`.`lakeflow`.`job_task_run_timeline`
      WHERE period_start_time >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE)
        AND period_end_time < CAST(date_add(:time_range.max, 1) AS TIMESTAMP)
        AND compute_ids IS NOT NULL
        AND size(compute_ids) > 0
    ) t
  ),
  -- Per cluster and hour: DBUs and $ billed.
  cluster_hours AS (
    SELECT u.workspace_id, u.usage_metadata.cluster_id AS cluster_id,
           date_trunc('HOUR', u.usage_start_time)            AS usage_hour,
           SUM(u.usage_quantity)                            AS net_dbus,
           SUM(u.usage_quantity * COALESCE(p.list_rate, 0)) AS est_usd_list
    FROM `system`.`billing`.`usage` u
    LEFT JOIN price p
      ON u.sku_name = p.sku_name AND u.cloud = p.cloud AND u.usage_unit = p.usage_unit
     AND u.usage_end_time >= p.price_start_time
     AND (p.price_end_time IS NULL OR u.usage_end_time < p.price_end_time)
    WHERE upper(u.usage_unit) = 'DBU'
      AND u.usage_metadata.cluster_id IS NOT NULL
      AND u.usage_date >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE)
      AND u.usage_date <  date_add(:time_range.max, 1)
    GROUP BY u.workspace_id, u.usage_metadata.cluster_id, date_trunc('HOUR', u.usage_start_time)
  ),
  -- When billing has no job id: the cluster's $ in the hours the job ran on it.
  hour_cost AS (
    SELECT th.workspace_id, th.job_id, th.compute_id,
           SUM(ch.net_dbus) AS net_dbus, SUM(ch.est_usd_list) AS est_usd_list
    FROM task_hours th
    JOIN cluster_hours ch
      ON ch.workspace_id = th.workspace_id AND ch.cluster_id = th.compute_id AND ch.usage_hour = th.usage_hour
    GROUP BY th.workspace_id, th.job_id, th.compute_id
  ),
  -- Per job and cluster: the cluster's source (UI, API, JOB, ...), task runs and $.
  finding AS (
    SELECT tc.workspace_id,
           CAST(tc.job_id AS STRING)         AS job_id,
           CAST(tc.compute_id AS STRING)     AS compute_id,
           COALESCE(c.cluster_source, CASE WHEN wc.compute_id IS NOT NULL THEN 'WAREHOUSE' END) AS cluster_source,
           COUNT(DISTINCT tc.run_id)          AS task_runs,
           COALESCE(MAX(cr.net_dbus),
                    CASE WHEN c.cluster_source IN ('UI', 'API') THEN MAX(hc.net_dbus) END, 0)     AS net_dbus,
           COALESCE(MAX(cr.est_usd_list),
                    CASE WHEN c.cluster_source IN ('UI', 'API') THEN MAX(hc.est_usd_list) END, 0) AS est_usd_list,
           COALESCE(MAX(cr.price_basis), 'priced') AS price_basis,
           CASE WHEN MAX(cr.est_usd_list) IS NOT NULL THEN 'job_metered'
                WHEN c.cluster_source IN ('UI', 'API') AND MAX(hc.est_usd_list) IS NOT NULL THEN 'cluster_hours'
           END                                AS cost_basis,
           MAX(cj.jobs_sharing_cluster)       AS jobs_sharing_cluster
    FROM task_compute tc
    LEFT JOIN (
      SELECT workspace_id, cluster_id, cluster_source
      FROM `system`.`compute`.`clusters`
      QUALIFY ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY workspace_id, cluster_id ORDER BY change_time DESC) = 1
    ) c
      ON tc.workspace_id = c.workspace_id AND tc.compute_id = c.cluster_id
    LEFT JOIN warehouse_compute wc
      ON tc.workspace_id = wc.workspace_id AND tc.compute_id = wc.compute_id
    LEFT JOIN cost_rollup cr
      ON tc.workspace_id = cr.workspace_id AND tc.compute_id = cr.cluster_id AND tc.job_id = cr.job_id
    LEFT JOIN hour_cost hc
      ON tc.workspace_id = hc.workspace_id AND tc.compute_id = hc.compute_id AND tc.job_id = hc.job_id
    LEFT JOIN cluster_jobs cj
      ON tc.workspace_id = cj.workspace_id AND tc.compute_id = cj.compute_id
    GROUP BY tc.workspace_id, tc.job_id, tc.compute_id, c.cluster_source, wc.compute_id
  )
  SELECT * FROM (
    SELECT
      workspace_id, job_id, compute_id, cluster_source, task_runs,
      net_dbus, est_usd_list, price_basis, cost_basis, jobs_sharing_cluster,
      -- kept under its old name (the app's discount/column logic reads it) - now just this job's
      -- own metered $ (est_usd_list), not a further even split.
      est_usd_list                                                           AS est_usd_list_share,
      CASE
        WHEN cluster_source IS NULL THEN 'NOT_ASSESSED'
        WHEN cluster_source IN ('UI', 'API') AND est_usd_list >= 100 THEN 'CRITICAL'
        WHEN cluster_source IN ('UI', 'API') THEN 'WARN'
        ELSE 'OK'
      END AS status
    FROM finding
    UNION ALL
    SELECT
      workspace_id,
      CAST(NULL AS STRING)                       AS job_id,
      CAST(NULL AS STRING)                       AS compute_id,
      'null or empty compute_ids'                AS cluster_source,
      dropped_runs                               AS task_runs,
      CAST(NULL AS DOUBLE)                       AS net_dbus,
      CAST(NULL AS DOUBLE)                       AS est_usd_list,
      CAST(NULL AS STRING)                       AS price_basis,
      CAST(NULL AS STRING)                       AS cost_basis,
      CAST(NULL AS BIGINT)                       AS jobs_sharing_cluster,
      CAST(NULL AS DOUBLE)                       AS est_usd_list_share,
      'NOT_ASSESSED'                             AS status
    FROM dropped
  ) placements
  ORDER BY
    CASE status WHEN 'CRITICAL' THEN 0 WHEN 'WARN' THEN 1 WHEN 'NOT_ASSESSED' THEN 2 ELSE 3 END,
    est_usd_list_share DESC
  LIMIT 100000
  ) q
)
  2. ws: Workspace id to name.
    Its SQL (5 lines)
    -- Workspace id to name.
ws AS (
  SELECT CAST(workspace_id AS STRING) AS workspace_id, workspace_name
  FROM system.access.workspaces_latest
)
  3. jb: Job id to name: the latest row per job.
    Its SQL (6 lines)
    -- Job id to name: the latest row per job.
jb AS (
  SELECT CAST(workspace_id AS STRING) AS workspace_id, CAST(job_id AS STRING) AS job_id, name
  FROM system.lakeflow.jobs
  QUALIFY ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY workspace_id, job_id ORDER BY change_time DESC) = 1
)
  4. cl: Cluster id to name and workspace: the latest row per cluster.
    Its SQL (6 lines)
    -- Cluster id to name and workspace: the latest row per cluster.
cl AS (
  SELECT CAST(cluster_id AS STRING) AS cluster_id, CAST(workspace_id AS STRING) AS workspace_id, cluster_name
  FROM system.compute.clusters
  QUALIFY ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY cluster_id ORDER BY change_time DESC) = 1
)
  5. a: Workspace, job and cluster names added; rows without a job left out.
    Its SQL (10 lines)
    -- Workspace, job and cluster names added; rows without a job left out.
a AS (
  SELECT a.*, COALESCE(ws.workspace_name, CAST(a.workspace_id AS STRING), 'Account-level') AS workspace,
         COALESCE(jb.name, a.job_id) AS job_name, COALESCE(cl.cluster_name, a.compute_id) AS cluster_name
  FROM ap a
  LEFT JOIN ws ON ws.workspace_id = CAST(a.workspace_id AS STRING)
  LEFT JOIN jb ON jb.workspace_id = CAST(a.workspace_id AS STRING) AND jb.job_id = a.job_id
  LEFT JOIN cl ON cl.cluster_id = a.compute_id
  WHERE a.job_id IS NOT NULL
)

3. Final query

One row per job and cluster, worst first.

4. Columns it returns

ColumnMeaning
jobThe job's name and id, as name (id). One-off submitted runs have no name and show the id.
clusterThe all-purpose cluster's name and id.
workspaceThe workspace's name (its id when the name is unknown).
statusCRITICAL, WARN, OK or NOT_ASSESSED (not enough data to judge), from the rule in Rules.
cluster_sourceUI or API: made by hand, not a job cluster.
task_runsTask runs on it.
total_usdThe job's $ on it at list price.
jobs_sharing_clusterHow many jobs use the same cluster.
flagged1 when status is CRITICAL or WARN, else 0; tiles add it up to count flagged rows.
SQL with comments (246 lines)
-- Jobs on all-purpose clusters. List price; dates from the Time Window.
-- The shared check lakeflow_jobs_on_all_purpose. Jobs on all-purpose clusters: per job and cluster, task runs and the job's own $.
WITH ap AS (
  SELECT datediff(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), :time_range.min) AS window_days, q.*
  FROM (
  -- Every task run in the window and each cluster it ran on.
  WITH task_compute AS (
    SELECT workspace_id, job_id, run_id, task_key, EXPLODE(compute_ids) AS compute_id
    FROM `system`.`lakeflow`.`job_task_run_timeline`
    WHERE period_start_time >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE)
      AND period_end_time < CAST(date_add(:time_range.max, 1) AS TIMESTAMP)
      AND result_state IS NOT NULL
      AND compute_ids IS NOT NULL
      AND size(compute_ids) > 0
  ),
  -- Runs that ran on serverless.
  serverless_runs AS (
    SELECT DISTINCT workspace_id, run_id
    FROM (
      SELECT workspace_id, run_id, EXPLODE(compute) AS c
      FROM `system`.`lakeflow`.`job_task_run_timeline`
      WHERE period_start_time >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE)
        AND period_end_time < CAST(date_add(:time_range.max, 1) AS TIMESTAMP)
        AND result_state IS NOT NULL
        AND compute IS NOT NULL
        AND size(compute) > 0
    ) exploded
    WHERE upper(c.type) = 'SERVERLESS'
  ),
  -- Per workspace: runs with no compute recorded and not serverless (can't be judged).
  dropped AS (
    SELECT t.workspace_id, COUNT(DISTINCT t.run_id) AS dropped_runs
    FROM `system`.`lakeflow`.`job_task_run_timeline` t
    LEFT JOIN serverless_runs s ON s.workspace_id = t.workspace_id AND s.run_id = t.run_id
    WHERE t.period_start_time >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE)
      AND t.period_end_time < CAST(date_add(:time_range.max, 1) AS TIMESTAMP)
      AND t.result_state IS NOT NULL
      AND (t.compute_ids IS NULL OR size(t.compute_ids) = 0)
      AND s.run_id IS NULL
    GROUP BY t.workspace_id
  ),
  -- Compute ids that are SQL warehouses, not clusters.
  warehouse_compute AS (
    SELECT DISTINCT workspace_id, w.warehouse_id AS compute_id
    FROM (
      SELECT workspace_id, EXPLODE(compute) AS w
      FROM `system`.`lakeflow`.`job_task_run_timeline`
      WHERE period_start_time >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE)
        AND period_end_time < CAST(date_add(:time_range.max, 1) AS TIMESTAMP)
        AND result_state IS NOT NULL
        AND compute IS NOT NULL
        AND size(compute) > 0
    ) exploded
    WHERE w.warehouse_id IS NOT NULL
  ),
  -- Per cluster: how many different jobs ran on it.
  cluster_jobs AS (
    SELECT workspace_id, compute_id, COUNT(DISTINCT job_id) AS jobs_sharing_cluster
    FROM task_compute
    GROUP BY workspace_id, compute_id
  ),
  -- List price of every SKU over time, with the date each price really ended.
  price AS (
    SELECT sku_name, cloud, usage_unit, price_start_time, price_end_time,
           CAST(pricing.effective_list.default AS DOUBLE) AS list_rate
    FROM 
  (
      SELECT account_id, sku_name, cloud, currency_code, usage_unit, pricing,
             price_start_time, effective_end AS price_end_time
      FROM (
          SELECT account_id, sku_name, cloud, currency_code, usage_unit, pricing,
                 price_start_time, price_end_time, next_start_time,
                 -- CASE, not LEAST, so a NULL end/next behaves the same on DuckDB and Databricks.
                 CASE
                     WHEN price_end_time IS NULL THEN next_start_time
                     WHEN next_start_time IS NULL THEN price_end_time
                     WHEN price_end_time <= next_start_time THEN price_end_time
                     ELSE next_start_time
                 END AS effective_end
          FROM (
              SELECT account_id, sku_name, cloud, currency_code, usage_unit, pricing,
                     price_start_time, price_end_time,
                     LEAD(price_start_time) OVER (
                         PARTITION BY sku_name, cloud, usage_unit
                         ORDER BY price_start_time, price_end_time NULLS LAST
                     ) AS next_start_time
              FROM `system`.`billing`.`list_prices`
              WHERE currency_code = 'USD'
          ) ranked
      ) capped
      WHERE effective_end IS NULL OR effective_end > price_start_time
  )
   list_prices
  ),
  -- Per cluster and job: the job's own billed DBUs and $ on that cluster.
  cost_rollup AS (
    -- Pre-aggregated per (cluster, job) over the SAME window as the finding, from usage_metadata.
    -- job_id: this job's OWN billed usage on the cluster, not the cluster's whole bill - notebook
    -- or another job's usage on the same shared cluster carries a different (or no) job_id and is
    -- excluded here, never folded into this job's row.
    SELECT u.workspace_id,
           u.usage_metadata.cluster_id                      AS cluster_id,
           u.usage_metadata.job_id                           AS job_id,
           SUM(u.usage_quantity)                            AS net_dbus,
           SUM(u.usage_quantity * COALESCE(p.list_rate, 0)) AS est_usd_list,
           CASE
             WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN p.list_rate IS NULL AND upper(u.sku_name) NOT LIKE '%FREE_USAGE%'
                           THEN u.usage_quantity ELSE 0 END) > 0 THEN 'unpriced'
             WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN p.list_rate IS NOT NULL THEN u.usage_quantity ELSE 0 END) = 0 THEN 'free'
             ELSE 'priced'
           END                                               AS price_basis
    FROM `system`.`billing`.`usage` u
    LEFT JOIN price p
      ON u.sku_name = p.sku_name AND u.cloud = p.cloud AND u.usage_unit = p.usage_unit
     AND u.usage_end_time >= p.price_start_time
     AND (p.price_end_time IS NULL OR u.usage_end_time < p.price_end_time)
    WHERE upper(u.usage_unit) = 'DBU'
      AND u.usage_metadata.cluster_id IS NOT NULL
      AND u.usage_metadata.job_id IS NOT NULL
      AND u.usage_date >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE)
      AND u.usage_date <  date_add(:time_range.max, 1)
    GROUP BY u.workspace_id, u.usage_metadata.cluster_id, u.usage_metadata.job_id
  ),
  -- The hours each job had a task running on each cluster.
  task_hours AS (
    SELECT DISTINCT workspace_id, job_id, compute_id, date_trunc('HOUR', period_start_time) AS usage_hour
    FROM (
      SELECT workspace_id, job_id, period_start_time, EXPLODE(compute_ids) AS compute_id
      FROM `system`.`lakeflow`.`job_task_run_timeline`
      WHERE period_start_time >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE)
        AND period_end_time < CAST(date_add(:time_range.max, 1) AS TIMESTAMP)
        AND compute_ids IS NOT NULL
        AND size(compute_ids) > 0
    ) t
  ),
  -- Per cluster and hour: DBUs and $ billed.
  cluster_hours AS (
    SELECT u.workspace_id, u.usage_metadata.cluster_id AS cluster_id,
           date_trunc('HOUR', u.usage_start_time)            AS usage_hour,
           SUM(u.usage_quantity)                            AS net_dbus,
           SUM(u.usage_quantity * COALESCE(p.list_rate, 0)) AS est_usd_list
    FROM `system`.`billing`.`usage` u
    LEFT JOIN price p
      ON u.sku_name = p.sku_name AND u.cloud = p.cloud AND u.usage_unit = p.usage_unit
     AND u.usage_end_time >= p.price_start_time
     AND (p.price_end_time IS NULL OR u.usage_end_time < p.price_end_time)
    WHERE upper(u.usage_unit) = 'DBU'
      AND u.usage_metadata.cluster_id IS NOT NULL
      AND u.usage_date >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE)
      AND u.usage_date <  date_add(:time_range.max, 1)
    GROUP BY u.workspace_id, u.usage_metadata.cluster_id, date_trunc('HOUR', u.usage_start_time)
  ),
  -- When billing has no job id: the cluster's $ in the hours the job ran on it.
  hour_cost AS (
    SELECT th.workspace_id, th.job_id, th.compute_id,
           SUM(ch.net_dbus) AS net_dbus, SUM(ch.est_usd_list) AS est_usd_list
    FROM task_hours th
    JOIN cluster_hours ch
      ON ch.workspace_id = th.workspace_id AND ch.cluster_id = th.compute_id AND ch.usage_hour = th.usage_hour
    GROUP BY th.workspace_id, th.job_id, th.compute_id
  ),
  -- Per job and cluster: the cluster's source (UI, API, JOB, ...), task runs and $.
  finding AS (
    SELECT tc.workspace_id,
           CAST(tc.job_id AS STRING)         AS job_id,
           CAST(tc.compute_id AS STRING)     AS compute_id,
           COALESCE(c.cluster_source, CASE WHEN wc.compute_id IS NOT NULL THEN 'WAREHOUSE' END) AS cluster_source,
           COUNT(DISTINCT tc.run_id)          AS task_runs,
           COALESCE(MAX(cr.net_dbus),
                    CASE WHEN c.cluster_source IN ('UI', 'API') THEN MAX(hc.net_dbus) END, 0)     AS net_dbus,
           COALESCE(MAX(cr.est_usd_list),
                    CASE WHEN c.cluster_source IN ('UI', 'API') THEN MAX(hc.est_usd_list) END, 0) AS est_usd_list,
           COALESCE(MAX(cr.price_basis), 'priced') AS price_basis,
           CASE WHEN MAX(cr.est_usd_list) IS NOT NULL THEN 'job_metered'
                WHEN c.cluster_source IN ('UI', 'API') AND MAX(hc.est_usd_list) IS NOT NULL THEN 'cluster_hours'
           END                                AS cost_basis,
           MAX(cj.jobs_sharing_cluster)       AS jobs_sharing_cluster
    FROM task_compute tc
    LEFT JOIN (
      SELECT workspace_id, cluster_id, cluster_source
      FROM `system`.`compute`.`clusters`
      QUALIFY ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY workspace_id, cluster_id ORDER BY change_time DESC) = 1
    ) c
      ON tc.workspace_id = c.workspace_id AND tc.compute_id = c.cluster_id
    LEFT JOIN warehouse_compute wc
      ON tc.workspace_id = wc.workspace_id AND tc.compute_id = wc.compute_id
    LEFT JOIN cost_rollup cr
      ON tc.workspace_id = cr.workspace_id AND tc.compute_id = cr.cluster_id AND tc.job_id = cr.job_id
    LEFT JOIN hour_cost hc
      ON tc.workspace_id = hc.workspace_id AND tc.compute_id = hc.compute_id AND tc.job_id = hc.job_id
    LEFT JOIN cluster_jobs cj
      ON tc.workspace_id = cj.workspace_id AND tc.compute_id = cj.compute_id
    GROUP BY tc.workspace_id, tc.job_id, tc.compute_id, c.cluster_source, wc.compute_id
  )
  SELECT * FROM (
    SELECT
      workspace_id, job_id, compute_id, cluster_source, task_runs,
      net_dbus, est_usd_list, price_basis, cost_basis, jobs_sharing_cluster,
      -- kept under its old name (the app's discount/column logic reads it) - now just this job's
      -- own metered $ (est_usd_list), not a further even split.
      est_usd_list                                                           AS est_usd_list_share,
      CASE
        WHEN cluster_source IS NULL THEN 'NOT_ASSESSED'
        WHEN cluster_source IN ('UI', 'API') AND est_usd_list >= 100 THEN 'CRITICAL'
        WHEN cluster_source IN ('UI', 'API') THEN 'WARN'
        ELSE 'OK'
      END AS status
    FROM finding
    UNION ALL
    SELECT
      workspace_id,
      CAST(NULL AS STRING)                       AS job_id,
      CAST(NULL AS STRING)                       AS compute_id,
      'null or empty compute_ids'                AS cluster_source,
      dropped_runs                               AS task_runs,
      CAST(NULL AS DOUBLE)                       AS net_dbus,
      CAST(NULL AS DOUBLE)                       AS est_usd_list,
      CAST(NULL AS STRING)                       AS price_basis,
      CAST(NULL AS STRING)                       AS cost_basis,
      CAST(NULL AS BIGINT)                       AS jobs_sharing_cluster,
      CAST(NULL AS DOUBLE)                       AS est_usd_list_share,
      'NOT_ASSESSED'                             AS status
    FROM dropped
  ) placements
  ORDER BY
    CASE status WHEN 'CRITICAL' THEN 0 WHEN 'WARN' THEN 1 WHEN 'NOT_ASSESSED' THEN 2 ELSE 3 END,
    est_usd_list_share DESC
  LIMIT 100000
  ) q
),
-- Workspace id to name.
ws AS (
  SELECT CAST(workspace_id AS STRING) AS workspace_id, workspace_name
  FROM system.access.workspaces_latest
),
-- Job id to name: the latest row per job.
jb AS (
  SELECT CAST(workspace_id AS STRING) AS workspace_id, CAST(job_id AS STRING) AS job_id, name
  FROM system.lakeflow.jobs
  QUALIFY ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY workspace_id, job_id ORDER BY change_time DESC) = 1
),
-- Cluster id to name and workspace: the latest row per cluster.
cl AS (
  SELECT CAST(cluster_id AS STRING) AS cluster_id, CAST(workspace_id AS STRING) AS workspace_id, cluster_name
  FROM system.compute.clusters
  QUALIFY ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY cluster_id ORDER BY change_time DESC) = 1
),
-- Workspace, job and cluster names added; rows without a job left out.
a AS (
  SELECT a.*, COALESCE(ws.workspace_name, CAST(a.workspace_id AS STRING), 'Account-level') AS workspace,
         COALESCE(jb.name, a.job_id) AS job_name, COALESCE(cl.cluster_name, a.compute_id) AS cluster_name
  FROM ap a
  LEFT JOIN ws ON ws.workspace_id = CAST(a.workspace_id AS STRING)
  LEFT JOIN jb ON jb.workspace_id = CAST(a.workspace_id AS STRING) AND jb.job_id = a.job_id
  LEFT JOIN cl ON cl.cluster_id = a.compute_id
  WHERE a.job_id IS NOT NULL
)
-- Final SELECT: the rows the widgets read.
SELECT concat(job_name, ' (', job_id, ')') AS job, concat(cluster_name, ' (', compute_id, ')') AS cluster,
       workspace, status, cluster_source, task_runs, est_usd_list AS total_usd, jobs_sharing_cluster,
       CASE WHEN status IN ('CRITICAL', 'WARN') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS flagged
FROM a
ORDER BY CASE status WHEN 'CRITICAL' THEN 0 WHEN 'WARN' THEN 1 ELSE 2 END, total_usd DESC NULLS LAST

Pipelines: compute billed outside update time

Shows: Per pipeline: DBUs billed in hours with no update running (the idle tail). Used by: The pipelines table on Flagged, not priced.

Why it is built this way: A pipeline's compute billed when no update runs is idle tail: the cluster stays up after the work is done.

How its tables join

Grey: system tables. Blue border: a shared check (its steps are listed under Steps). White: steps of the query. Green: the rows the dashboard shows. ◆ marks the columns the joins use.

  1. lakeflow_pipeline_idle_tail_duration → p: the check's rows
  2. pl → p: LEFT JOIN ON workspace_id, pipeline_id: names
  3. ws → p: LEFT JOIN ON workspace_id: names

1. System tables it reads

  • system.access.workspaces_latest
  • system.billing.list_prices
  • system.billing.usage
  • system.lakeflow.pipeline_update_timeline
  • system.lakeflow.pipelines

2. Steps (CTEs), in order

  1. pipes: The shared check lakeflow_pipeline_idle_tail_duration. Pipeline idle tail: per pipeline, DBUs billed in hours with no update running.
    1. price: List price of every SKU over time, with the date each price really ended.
    2. cost_rollup: Per pipeline: DBUs and $ billed in the window.
    3. billed_hours: Every billed row of a pipeline in the window.
    4. billed_active: Whether an update was running during each billed row; serverless never lingers.
    5. idle: Per pipeline: DBUs billed with no update running.
    Its SQL (125 lines)
    -- Pipelines: compute billed outside update time. List price; dates from the Time Window.
-- The shared check lakeflow_pipeline_idle_tail_duration. Pipeline idle tail: per pipeline, DBUs billed in hours with no update running.
pipes AS (
  SELECT datediff(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), :time_range.min) AS window_days, q.*
  FROM (
  -- List price of every SKU over time, with the date each price really ended.
  WITH price AS (
    SELECT sku_name, cloud, usage_unit, price_start_time, price_end_time,
           CAST(pricing.effective_list.default AS DOUBLE) AS list_rate
    FROM 
  (
      SELECT account_id, sku_name, cloud, currency_code, usage_unit, pricing,
             price_start_time, effective_end AS price_end_time
      FROM (
          SELECT account_id, sku_name, cloud, currency_code, usage_unit, pricing,
                 price_start_time, price_end_time, next_start_time,
                 -- CASE, not LEAST, so a NULL end/next behaves the same on DuckDB and Databricks.
                 CASE
                     WHEN price_end_time IS NULL THEN next_start_time
                     WHEN next_start_time IS NULL THEN price_end_time
                     WHEN price_end_time <= next_start_time THEN price_end_time
                     ELSE next_start_time
                 END AS effective_end
          FROM (
              SELECT account_id, sku_name, cloud, currency_code, usage_unit, pricing,
                     price_start_time, price_end_time,
                     LEAD(price_start_time) OVER (
                         PARTITION BY sku_name, cloud, usage_unit
                         ORDER BY price_start_time, price_end_time NULLS LAST
                     ) AS next_start_time
              FROM `system`.`billing`.`list_prices`
              WHERE currency_code = 'USD'
          ) ranked
      ) capped
      WHERE effective_end IS NULL OR effective_end > price_start_time
  )
   list_prices
  ),
  -- Per pipeline: DBUs and $ billed in the window.
  cost_rollup AS (
    SELECT u.workspace_id,
           u.usage_metadata.dlt_pipeline_id AS dlt_pipeline_id,
           SUM(u.usage_quantity)                            AS net_dbus,
           SUM(u.usage_quantity * COALESCE(p.list_rate, 0)) AS est_usd_list,
           CASE
             WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN p.list_rate IS NULL AND upper(u.sku_name) NOT LIKE '%FREE_USAGE%'
                           THEN u.usage_quantity ELSE 0 END) > 0 THEN 'unpriced'
             WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN p.list_rate IS NOT NULL THEN u.usage_quantity ELSE 0 END) = 0 THEN 'free'
             ELSE 'priced'
           END                                               AS price_basis
    FROM `system`.`billing`.`usage` u
    LEFT JOIN price p
      ON u.sku_name = p.sku_name AND u.cloud = p.cloud AND u.usage_unit = p.usage_unit
     AND u.usage_end_time >= p.price_start_time
     AND (p.price_end_time IS NULL OR u.usage_end_time < p.price_end_time)
    WHERE upper(u.usage_unit) = 'DBU'
      AND u.usage_metadata.dlt_pipeline_id IS NOT NULL
      AND u.usage_date >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE)
      AND u.usage_date <  date_add(:time_range.max, 1)
    GROUP BY u.workspace_id, u.usage_metadata.dlt_pipeline_id
  ),
  -- Every billed row of a pipeline in the window.
  billed_hours AS (
    SELECT u.workspace_id, u.usage_metadata.dlt_pipeline_id AS pipeline_id, u.record_id,
           u.usage_start_time, u.usage_end_time, u.usage_quantity,
           COALESCE(u.product_features.is_serverless, FALSE) AS serverless
    FROM `system`.`billing`.`usage` u
    WHERE upper(u.usage_unit) = 'DBU'
      AND u.usage_metadata.dlt_pipeline_id IS NOT NULL
      AND u.usage_date >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE)
      AND u.usage_date <  date_add(:time_range.max, 1)
  ),
  -- Whether an update was running during each billed row; serverless never lingers.
  billed_active AS (
    -- whether any update slice overlaps the billed row's hour; serverless rows never linger
    SELECT b.workspace_id, b.pipeline_id, b.record_id, b.usage_quantity, b.serverless,
           MAX(CASE WHEN s.pipeline_id IS NOT NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS overlapped
    FROM billed_hours b
    LEFT JOIN `system`.`lakeflow`.`pipeline_update_timeline` s
      ON s.workspace_id = b.workspace_id AND s.pipeline_id = b.pipeline_id
     AND s.period_start_time < b.usage_end_time AND s.period_end_time > b.usage_start_time
    GROUP BY b.workspace_id, b.pipeline_id, b.record_id, b.usage_quantity, b.serverless
  ),
  -- Per pipeline: DBUs billed with no update running.
  idle AS (
    SELECT workspace_id, pipeline_id,
           SUM(CASE WHEN overlapped = 0 AND NOT serverless THEN usage_quantity ELSE 0 END) AS idle_dbus
    FROM billed_active
    GROUP BY workspace_id, pipeline_id
  )
  SELECT u.workspace_id, u.pipeline_id, p.pipeline_type, p.setting_continuous, p.setting_development,
         COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN u.result_state IS NOT NULL THEN u.update_id END) AS updates,
         -- every hourly slice counts: an update over an hour writes one row per hour, the result on the last
         SUM(unix_timestamp(u.period_end_time) - unix_timestamp(u.period_start_time)) AS active_seconds_total,
         COALESCE(MAX(cr.net_dbus), 0)     AS net_dbus,
         COALESCE(MAX(cr.est_usd_list), 0) AS est_usd_list,
         COALESCE(MAX(cr.price_basis), 'priced') AS price_basis,
         COALESCE(MAX(i.idle_dbus), 0) AS idle_dbus,
         MAX(i.idle_dbus) / NULLIF(MAX(cr.net_dbus), 0) AS idle_share,
         -- status: worst-first band on DBUs billed outside update time (field heuristic; 100 / 500).
         CASE
           WHEN p.setting_continuous = true THEN 'NOT_ASSESSED'
           WHEN COALESCE(MAX(i.idle_dbus), 0) >= 500 THEN 'CRITICAL'
           WHEN COALESCE(MAX(i.idle_dbus), 0) >= 100 THEN 'WARN'
           ELSE 'OK'
         END AS status
  FROM `system`.`lakeflow`.`pipeline_update_timeline` u
  LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT workspace_id, pipeline_id, pipeline_type,
           settings.continuous  AS setting_continuous,
           settings.development AS setting_development
    FROM `system`.`lakeflow`.`pipelines`
    QUALIFY ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY workspace_id, pipeline_id ORDER BY change_time DESC) = 1
  ) p
    ON u.workspace_id = p.workspace_id AND u.pipeline_id = p.pipeline_id
  LEFT JOIN cost_rollup cr
    ON u.workspace_id = cr.workspace_id AND u.pipeline_id = cr.dlt_pipeline_id
  LEFT JOIN idle i
    ON u.workspace_id = i.workspace_id AND u.pipeline_id = i.pipeline_id
  WHERE u.period_start_time >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE)
    AND u.period_end_time < CAST(date_add(:time_range.max, 1) AS TIMESTAMP)
  GROUP BY u.workspace_id, u.pipeline_id, p.pipeline_type, p.setting_continuous, p.setting_development
  ORDER BY idle_dbus DESC, net_dbus DESC
  ) q
)
  2. ws: Workspace id to name.
    Its SQL (5 lines)
    -- Workspace id to name.
ws AS (
  SELECT CAST(workspace_id AS STRING) AS workspace_id, workspace_name
  FROM system.access.workspaces_latest
)
  3. pl: Pipeline id to name: the latest row per pipeline.
    Its SQL (6 lines)
    -- Pipeline id to name: the latest row per pipeline.
pl AS (
  SELECT CAST(workspace_id AS STRING) AS workspace_id, CAST(pipeline_id AS STRING) AS pipeline_id, name
  FROM system.lakeflow.pipelines
  QUALIFY ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY workspace_id, pipeline_id ORDER BY change_time DESC) = 1
)
  4. p: Workspace and pipeline names added.
    Its SQL (7 lines)
    -- Workspace and pipeline names added.
p AS (
  SELECT p.*, COALESCE(ws.workspace_name, CAST(p.workspace_id AS STRING), 'Account-level') AS workspace, COALESCE(pl.name, CAST(p.pipeline_id AS STRING)) AS pipeline_name
  FROM pipes p
  LEFT JOIN ws ON ws.workspace_id = CAST(p.workspace_id AS STRING)
  LEFT JOIN pl ON pl.workspace_id = CAST(p.workspace_id AS STRING) AND pl.pipeline_id = CAST(p.pipeline_id AS STRING)
)

3. Final query

One row per pipeline, most idle DBUs first.

4. Columns it returns

ColumnMeaning
pipelineThe pipeline's name and id.
workspaceThe workspace's name (its id when the name is unknown).
statusCRITICAL, WARN, OK or NOT_ASSESSED (not enough data to judge), from the rule in Rules.
updatesUpdates in the window.
idle_dbusDBUs billed in hours with no update running.
idle_pctidle_dbus as a share of its DBUs.
total_usdIts $ at list price.
flagged1 when status is CRITICAL or WARN, else 0; tiles add it up to count flagged rows.
SQL with comments (139 lines)
-- Pipelines: compute billed outside update time. List price; dates from the Time Window.
-- The shared check lakeflow_pipeline_idle_tail_duration. Pipeline idle tail: per pipeline, DBUs billed in hours with no update running.
WITH pipes AS (
  SELECT datediff(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), :time_range.min) AS window_days, q.*
  FROM (
  -- List price of every SKU over time, with the date each price really ended.
  WITH price AS (
    SELECT sku_name, cloud, usage_unit, price_start_time, price_end_time,
           CAST(pricing.effective_list.default AS DOUBLE) AS list_rate
    FROM 
  (
      SELECT account_id, sku_name, cloud, currency_code, usage_unit, pricing,
             price_start_time, effective_end AS price_end_time
      FROM (
          SELECT account_id, sku_name, cloud, currency_code, usage_unit, pricing,
                 price_start_time, price_end_time, next_start_time,
                 -- CASE, not LEAST, so a NULL end/next behaves the same on DuckDB and Databricks.
                 CASE
                     WHEN price_end_time IS NULL THEN next_start_time
                     WHEN next_start_time IS NULL THEN price_end_time
                     WHEN price_end_time <= next_start_time THEN price_end_time
                     ELSE next_start_time
                 END AS effective_end
          FROM (
              SELECT account_id, sku_name, cloud, currency_code, usage_unit, pricing,
                     price_start_time, price_end_time,
                     LEAD(price_start_time) OVER (
                         PARTITION BY sku_name, cloud, usage_unit
                         ORDER BY price_start_time, price_end_time NULLS LAST
                     ) AS next_start_time
              FROM `system`.`billing`.`list_prices`
              WHERE currency_code = 'USD'
          ) ranked
      ) capped
      WHERE effective_end IS NULL OR effective_end > price_start_time
  )
   list_prices
  ),
  -- Per pipeline: DBUs and $ billed in the window.
  cost_rollup AS (
    SELECT u.workspace_id,
           u.usage_metadata.dlt_pipeline_id AS dlt_pipeline_id,
           SUM(u.usage_quantity)                            AS net_dbus,
           SUM(u.usage_quantity * COALESCE(p.list_rate, 0)) AS est_usd_list,
           CASE
             WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN p.list_rate IS NULL AND upper(u.sku_name) NOT LIKE '%FREE_USAGE%'
                           THEN u.usage_quantity ELSE 0 END) > 0 THEN 'unpriced'
             WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN p.list_rate IS NOT NULL THEN u.usage_quantity ELSE 0 END) = 0 THEN 'free'
             ELSE 'priced'
           END                                               AS price_basis
    FROM `system`.`billing`.`usage` u
    LEFT JOIN price p
      ON u.sku_name = p.sku_name AND u.cloud = p.cloud AND u.usage_unit = p.usage_unit
     AND u.usage_end_time >= p.price_start_time
     AND (p.price_end_time IS NULL OR u.usage_end_time < p.price_end_time)
    WHERE upper(u.usage_unit) = 'DBU'
      AND u.usage_metadata.dlt_pipeline_id IS NOT NULL
      AND u.usage_date >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE)
      AND u.usage_date <  date_add(:time_range.max, 1)
    GROUP BY u.workspace_id, u.usage_metadata.dlt_pipeline_id
  ),
  -- Every billed row of a pipeline in the window.
  billed_hours AS (
    SELECT u.workspace_id, u.usage_metadata.dlt_pipeline_id AS pipeline_id, u.record_id,
           u.usage_start_time, u.usage_end_time, u.usage_quantity,
           COALESCE(u.product_features.is_serverless, FALSE) AS serverless
    FROM `system`.`billing`.`usage` u
    WHERE upper(u.usage_unit) = 'DBU'
      AND u.usage_metadata.dlt_pipeline_id IS NOT NULL
      AND u.usage_date >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE)
      AND u.usage_date <  date_add(:time_range.max, 1)
  ),
  -- Whether an update was running during each billed row; serverless never lingers.
  billed_active AS (
    -- whether any update slice overlaps the billed row's hour; serverless rows never linger
    SELECT b.workspace_id, b.pipeline_id, b.record_id, b.usage_quantity, b.serverless,
           MAX(CASE WHEN s.pipeline_id IS NOT NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS overlapped
    FROM billed_hours b
    LEFT JOIN `system`.`lakeflow`.`pipeline_update_timeline` s
      ON s.workspace_id = b.workspace_id AND s.pipeline_id = b.pipeline_id
     AND s.period_start_time < b.usage_end_time AND s.period_end_time > b.usage_start_time
    GROUP BY b.workspace_id, b.pipeline_id, b.record_id, b.usage_quantity, b.serverless
  ),
  -- Per pipeline: DBUs billed with no update running.
  idle AS (
    SELECT workspace_id, pipeline_id,
           SUM(CASE WHEN overlapped = 0 AND NOT serverless THEN usage_quantity ELSE 0 END) AS idle_dbus
    FROM billed_active
    GROUP BY workspace_id, pipeline_id
  )
  SELECT u.workspace_id, u.pipeline_id, p.pipeline_type, p.setting_continuous, p.setting_development,
         COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN u.result_state IS NOT NULL THEN u.update_id END) AS updates,
         -- every hourly slice counts: an update over an hour writes one row per hour, the result on the last
         SUM(unix_timestamp(u.period_end_time) - unix_timestamp(u.period_start_time)) AS active_seconds_total,
         COALESCE(MAX(cr.net_dbus), 0)     AS net_dbus,
         COALESCE(MAX(cr.est_usd_list), 0) AS est_usd_list,
         COALESCE(MAX(cr.price_basis), 'priced') AS price_basis,
         COALESCE(MAX(i.idle_dbus), 0) AS idle_dbus,
         MAX(i.idle_dbus) / NULLIF(MAX(cr.net_dbus), 0) AS idle_share,
         -- status: worst-first band on DBUs billed outside update time (field heuristic; 100 / 500).
         CASE
           WHEN p.setting_continuous = true THEN 'NOT_ASSESSED'
           WHEN COALESCE(MAX(i.idle_dbus), 0) >= 500 THEN 'CRITICAL'
           WHEN COALESCE(MAX(i.idle_dbus), 0) >= 100 THEN 'WARN'
           ELSE 'OK'
         END AS status
  FROM `system`.`lakeflow`.`pipeline_update_timeline` u
  LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT workspace_id, pipeline_id, pipeline_type,
           settings.continuous  AS setting_continuous,
           settings.development AS setting_development
    FROM `system`.`lakeflow`.`pipelines`
    QUALIFY ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY workspace_id, pipeline_id ORDER BY change_time DESC) = 1
  ) p
    ON u.workspace_id = p.workspace_id AND u.pipeline_id = p.pipeline_id
  LEFT JOIN cost_rollup cr
    ON u.workspace_id = cr.workspace_id AND u.pipeline_id = cr.dlt_pipeline_id
  LEFT JOIN idle i
    ON u.workspace_id = i.workspace_id AND u.pipeline_id = i.pipeline_id
  WHERE u.period_start_time >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE)
    AND u.period_end_time < CAST(date_add(:time_range.max, 1) AS TIMESTAMP)
  GROUP BY u.workspace_id, u.pipeline_id, p.pipeline_type, p.setting_continuous, p.setting_development
  ORDER BY idle_dbus DESC, net_dbus DESC
  ) q
),
-- Workspace id to name.
ws AS (
  SELECT CAST(workspace_id AS STRING) AS workspace_id, workspace_name
  FROM system.access.workspaces_latest
),
-- Pipeline id to name: the latest row per pipeline.
pl AS (
  SELECT CAST(workspace_id AS STRING) AS workspace_id, CAST(pipeline_id AS STRING) AS pipeline_id, name
  FROM system.lakeflow.pipelines
  QUALIFY ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY workspace_id, pipeline_id ORDER BY change_time DESC) = 1
),
-- Workspace and pipeline names added.
p AS (
  SELECT p.*, COALESCE(ws.workspace_name, CAST(p.workspace_id AS STRING), 'Account-level') AS workspace, COALESCE(pl.name, CAST(p.pipeline_id AS STRING)) AS pipeline_name
  FROM pipes p
  LEFT JOIN ws ON ws.workspace_id = CAST(p.workspace_id AS STRING)
  LEFT JOIN pl ON pl.workspace_id = CAST(p.workspace_id AS STRING) AND pl.pipeline_id = CAST(p.pipeline_id AS STRING)
)
-- Final SELECT: the rows the widgets read.
SELECT concat(pipeline_name, ' (', pipeline_id, ')') AS pipeline, workspace, status, updates,
       ROUND(idle_dbus, 1) AS idle_dbus, ROUND(idle_share * 100, 1) AS idle_pct, est_usd_list AS total_usd,
       CASE WHEN status IN ('CRITICAL', 'WARN') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS flagged
FROM p
ORDER BY idle_dbus DESC
